| **T345** | Read validity: candle = 3 bars of its TF; pattern = 3 bars of the next TF; trend = 3 bars of the TF after that | **Yes** |
| **T346** | 5m/15m/1H stack forecasts 1–3 days; short-term target reached in 3h–1 day; re-read every ~3h | **Partly** — "1–2 days" vs "1–3 days" |
| **T347** | At a 50% line/base, ≥3 hours (1H) of wick-only probes with no body beyond → defending side wins, target overall 50% | **Yes** |
| **T348** | Body close beyond this TF's base → next target is the next-higher TF's base | **Yes** |
| **T349** | Each successive base break travels faster: ×2 (15m), ×3 (1H), ×4–5 (4H) | **Partly** — speed unit undefined; heard once |
| **T350** | Below 50% on D/4H/1H/15m/5m → price revisits the overall 50% before a deep further fall | **Yes** |
| **T351** | A later layer group that fails to exceed the prior layer's high = seller still in control | **Yes** |
| **T352** | No averaging into a falling market without a marked boundary AND visible volume/buyer involvement | **Partly** — "involvement" not quantified here |
| **T353** | Price inside the 5m/15m/1H 50% band and contracting → aggressive break; break needs an outside close beyond the side's previous push | **Yes** |

## Video 045 — Depth vs structure, failed candle/pattern, and the A-head / V-head rule

After checking the prior calls, he re-teaches the basics on a BTC chart: 25/50/75/100 bounces, wick vs body at a broken low, and a new pair of words — **depth** (same-direction extension) and **structure** (the opposite-direction move). He defines inside, outside and **failed** candles by where the next candle's body sits, the "failed pattern" (bounce short of 50%), and a sequence rule for breaking A-heads and V-heads. The second half is on training, fees, and building capital slowly.

### T354 — DEPTH = SAME-DIRECTION EXTENSION; STRUCTURE = THE OPPOSITE MOVE

*"From now on in the market, depth means the market following the same trend; structure means following the opposite trend. Depth first means 'deepness' … First time they brought this much depth, second time broke the low and brought this much, third time broke the low and brought this much. The fourth time it couldn't break the low and take it deep. This is how you must read the market."* (045 · 00:05:04 – 00:05:34)
*"Then the buyer's structure forms … does the structure form in proportion to the depth?"* (045 · 00:03:03, 00:09:04)

Vocabulary for later videos: depth = the leg that makes a new extreme in the trend's direction; structure = the counter-leg. "Structure proportional to depth" is the test *(ratio not given here)*.

### T355 — INSIDE / OUTSIDE / FAILED CANDLE (BY BODY OVERLAP)

*"There's a candle with a maximum high and low; the next candle touched inside this high and low and opened — this is called an inside candle. If the next candle came wholly outside without touching this high and low, not a single point touching the line — that is an outside candle."* (045 · 00:06:04 – 00:06:34)
*"What is a failed candle? If the candle that touches and opens has 75% of its body inside and only 25% outside, that is a failed candle. If it is 50-50 — 50% inside, 50% outside — it is not a failed candle; it means both are at equal force."* (045 · 00:07:04)
*"Full-bodied, not a failed candle — then you can write it down as confirmed that the market will go further down."* (045 · 00:03:33)

**Refines / partly contradicts T95**: here an *outside* candle is one whose body does not overlap the prior candle's high–low range at all, and a candle that pokes out with ≤25% of its body beyond the level is *failed*. That also sharpens T20 (≥50% beyond = a real break; 25% beyond = failed; 50/50 = equal force). The caption's "opened" vs "closed" wording is *(unclear)*.

### T356 — A NEW LOW BY WICK → RETRACE TO THE 25%; BY A FULL BODY → CONTINUATION

*"The first time, see, it only put a wick. That is why it went back to this 25 range again. The second time it aggressively put a body — strongly outside, full-bodied, not a failed candle — so the market will certainly go further down."* (045 · 00:03:03 – 00:03:33)

Links T97 (quarters) and T100: a break of the low by wick only is expected to bounce back to the 25% of the leg; a full-body (non-failed) outside close continues.

### T357 — FAILED PATTERN: A BOUNCE THAT NEVER REACHES 50% KEEPS THE SELLER IN CONTROL

*"Has it gone to the 50% of this? No — so this is a failed pattern. Next, could it go to 50%? No. Again? No … up to now the market is being maintained as a failed pattern, so it is fully in the seller's control."* (045 · 00:09:04 – 00:09:34)

Names what T106 calls a weak pattern; also the "Failed V" of T22. A run of failed patterns = seller trend.

### T358 — THE PRIOR CANDLE'S BODY–WICK ZONE: BODY INSIDE OK, WICK BEYOND OK, BODY BEYOND = NEXT BASE

*"However long it takes, the body may come inside this area — between the existing body and wick — and a wick may come beyond this area; both mean the market is still in the buyer's control. But the day a body is put outside beyond the existing body-wick, and aggressively, the market is likely to go further down."* (045 · 00:11:37 – 00:12:07)
*"If it breaks this and puts an outside body, the market will come to this base. Even if it comes in a single candle, don't be surprised — because the market is contracting."* (045 · 00:21:52)

Operational form of T100 using the extreme candle's body edge and wick edge as a two-line band.

### T359 — WICK-ONLY PROBES INTO THE ZONE = ~75% BUYER CONTROL; BODIES = ~50%

*"Even if a body came, it would mean 50% in the seller's control. But only wicks have come — so 75% the market has come into the buyer's control. One, two, three … almost seven times only wicks have come into this area; the buyer has strongly taken the market in hand."* (045 · 00:13:41) *(heard once)*

Quantifies T100. The 75/50 figures are his control estimates, not measured.

### T360 — A-HEAD / V-HEAD SEQUENCE

*"If it breaks the A pattern's head, it has strongly come into the buyer's control. Likewise if it breaks the V pattern's head and comes down, it has strongly come into the seller's control."* (045 · 00:26:53)
*"Touched it — what does breaking mean? It must put an outside candle beyond it."* (045 · 00:27:23)
*"It has gone inside the V pattern's head but must break the A pattern's head. If it is inside, the market is neutral — belongs to both … the market is contracting, so it will explode one way."* (045 · 00:28:23)
*"After seller control, the market can go inside this V pattern, but it must not break this A pattern's head. If it breaks, it comes back into the buyer's control."* (045 · 00:31:29)

Corroborates T99/T101 and adds the state machine: after a V-head break (seller), a retrace may enter the V but control only flips back if it closes outside the last A-head; between the two heads = neutral/contracting (T104).

### T361 — START SMALL, GROW THREE YEARS, WORK BY ALERTS

*"When you start this business, start with one or two times your monthly salary … keep growing it for three years … it will give five to ten times each month."* (045 · 00:59:03 – 00:59:34)
*"You don't need to put much time — 15 or 20 minutes a day in your free time is enough … set an alert at that place and go; when it comes, a message comes."* (045 · 01:00:04 – 01:00:34)

Corroborates T46 (own capital only). The 5–10× monthly figure is a claim with no stated base *(unclear)*. He also says in passing: not always the 5m chart, not always 1:2/1:3, and "with a stop loss everywhere you only get losses" (045 · 00:15:45 – 00:16:16) — corroborates T45.

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T354** | Depth = leg making a new extreme in trend direction; structure = counter-leg | **Yes** — definition |
| **T355** | Inside = overlaps prior range; outside = body wholly beyond prior range; failed = ≤25% of body beyond; 50/50 = equal force | **Yes** — conflicts with T95's close-beyond definition |
| **T356** | Low broken by wick only → bounce to 25% of leg; by full non-failed body → continuation | **Yes** |
| **T357** | Bounce < 50% of the pattern = failed pattern; consecutive failed patterns = seller control | **Yes** |
| **T358** | Prior extreme candle's body edge and wick edge form a band; a body close beyond the band → next base | **Yes** |
| **T359** | Repeated wick-only probes (≈7) into the band = ~75% buyer control | **Partly** — % is a label |
| **T360** | After a V-head outside break, control returns to buyer only on an outside close beyond the last A-head; between heads = neutral | **Yes** |
| **T361** | Seed with 1–2 months' salary, compound 3 years; 15–20 min/day via price alerts | **No** — lifestyle/claim |

## Video 046 — Long-term BTC (monthly → 5m): every move returns to its 50%, and how long it takes

On a monthly BTC chart he shows the move returning to its 50%, then states the "secret": any move retraces to its 50% within a time window tied to the move's own duration. He separates *structure* (~25%), *correction* (≤50%) and *crash* (>50%) with frequencies, describes greed-controlled trends where pullbacks only reach the prior body/wick ("reversible trades"), the 50% as a "resting point", and the three ways price meets a level. He closes with timeframe choice by product and a warning against a fixed 1:2 reward:risk.

### T362 — ON AVERAGE, EVERY THIRD TRADE MUST GROW THE CAPITAL

*"As I often said, every third trade must keep growing your capital. That is called a business. That doesn't mean the first and second will be losses — on average every third trade, your profit must be growing the capital."* (046 · 00:05:07 – 00:05:37)

A performance yardstick for the whole method, not an entry rule.

### T363 — MAIN BOUNDARY = 0 / 50 / 100; 25 AND 75 ARE SUB-BOUNDARIES

*"We call these sub-boundaries, not the main boundary. The main boundary is this zero line, the 100 line below, and the 50 line in the middle. 70 to 90% of the big investors and big traders follow these places; psychological changes happen here — control of fear ends and price of attraction begins."* (046 · 00:07:08)

Ranks the quarter lines of T97: decisions cluster at 0/50/100. Corroborates T343.

### T364 — EVERY MOVE RETURNS TO ITS 50%, WITHIN ⅓× TO 1.5× ITS OWN DURATION

*"However high the market goes, it will surely come to its 50%; likewise however low it comes, it will surely go to its 50%. This is the biggest secret in the market. But there is time for it. If it took three months, … at least one-third of it, one month, to at most one and a half times — three and half of three, four and a half months. At least one month to at most four and a half months it is likely to come to 50%."* (046 · 00:10:11 – 00:10:42)
*"About 100 months — so it must come within 30 months to 150 months, minimum to maximum. This one didn't take that long — it came in just four months."* (046 · 00:11:13)

The most testable time rule in the batch: for a completed leg lasting *D* bars, price revisits the leg's 50% between *D/3* and *1.5·D* bars later (his own example came faster than the minimum). Refines T73 (every level is retested) with a time window.

### T365 — STRUCTURE (~25%) vs CORRECTION (≤50%) vs CRASH (>50%)

*"Correction is like a flight — it must come down within a set time. Crash is falling vertically … a total collapse. This crash will surely happen within five to seven years — within 1.5 times the number of candles it took to rise, that bottom will come."* (046 · 00:13:15 – 00:13:45)
*"If it comes at least to about 50%, that is a correction; correction will surely happen within one to three years. Going below 50% is called a crash … Within 50% from the overall high is not a crash — no need to fear, those are corrections. Then 25% is an ordinary structure; it will surely happen every three months to one year."* (046 · 00:14:16 – 00:14:47)
*"In trading, structure keeps happening in one to three months; the next correction normally within three to six months; a crash in trading within six months to one year."* (046 · 00:14:47 – 00:15:18)

Classifies a pullback by depth as a fraction of the prior rise and gives a recurrence period per class (investment scale on D/W/M; shorter "trading" scale). Which chart the "trading" periods refer to is *(unclear)*.

### T366 — CONTROL OF FEAR / CONTROL OF GREED; "REVERSIBLE TRADE"

*"Not even one green candle comes … breaking this one's low it puts a low … the whole market is blood-red. This is called control of fear."* (046 · 00:11:43)
*"It went up and came back — to exactly that body-wick. Again went up, came back to the body-wick … This is control of greed: without coming down, in one stretch they lift it up."* (046 · 00:22:27 – 00:22:57)
*"This is called reversible. If you missed the market at a boundary, you can catch it as a reversible trade … it will not have come the full 50%; it comes to about 25% and goes."* (046 · 00:23:27)

In a greed-controlled trend each pullback only reaches the previous body/wick zone (~25%), giving re-entries; in a fear-controlled trend each candle breaks the prior low with no green candles.

### T367 — THE 50% LINE IS THE "RESTING POINT"

*"This 50% line is what I called the resting point … buyers try to touch the upper head, sellers the lower head; in that game the resting point they take in between is the 50% line. They will surely go at least up to that 50% line, then rest."* (046 · 00:29:00 – 00:29:32)
*"If the seller puts an outside candle beyond this candle's body-wick above, it will come to the body-wick below — or at least to its 50% and rest, and then decide whether to go down or up."* (046 · 00:42:45 – 00:43:17)

Minimum target after a control change: the 50% of the current range. Corroborates T26/T364.

### T368 — THREE WAYS PRICE MEETS A LEVEL

*"Sometimes it turns in advance of the 50%, sometimes delayed; it touches and turns, turns without touching, breaks and turns. The market does it three ways … turns without touching — comes near that place and turns."* (046 · 00:51:26 – 00:51:57)
*"That place: it will go without touching it, touch it and go, or break it and then U-turn and go up. That's all."* (046 · 01:06:15)

Any level test resolves as (a) reversal short of it, (b) touch-reversal, or (c) break-and-return. Matters for how T364/T367 targets are scored in a backtest (a miss of a few ticks is still a "turn").

### T369 — CHART SET BY PRODUCT AND HOLDING PERIOD

*"If you do monthly-expiry trades — how the market will be from a week to a month — see day, 4 hour, 1 hour. If you are a trader, scalping, intraday, at most weekly expiry — 5 minute, 15 minute, 1 hour. If you focus on NIFTY, Bank NIFTY — 1 minute, 5 minute, 15 minute plus 1 hour, add the 1 minute too, because it's micro short-term."* (046 · 00:39:10 – 00:39:41)
*"The market is active only 6 hours … in those six hours the first hour and the last hour are violent; the remaining four hours are silent."* (046 · 00:40:11)

Extends T337; the session-hours claim corroborates T93.

### T370 — HORIZON FROM ONE CHART (CANDLE / PATTERN / TREND) — SECOND VERSION

*"Take one 1-hour candle and analyse — the next three hours. A day candle — three days; a weekly candle — three weeks; a monthly candle — three months. Take a pattern: bigger than 1 hour is day, so one day to three days. A 1-hour trend: next bigger after day is week — one week to three weeks."* (046 · 00:48:22 – 00:48:53)
*"On the day chart, a trend/outline boundary — one month to three months; a pattern — one day to one week; a day candle — one day to three days."* (046 · 00:34:05 – 00:34:36)

**Conflicts with T345** (video 044): there the 1H pattern → 4H (12 h) and 1H trend → Day (3 days); here the 1H pattern → Day (1–3 days) and 1H trend → Week (1–3 weeks). The candle step (×3 bars) is the same in both.

### T371 — NO FIXED 1:2 REWARD:RISK

*"First trade you take a 10-point loss … if you keep doing 1:2 … if two losses are 20 points, then even with one profit you are still at a loss … plus brokerage. Sitting and doing 1:2 everywhere foolishly — how big a stupidity is that."* (046 · 00:59:07 – 00:59:37)
*"Look at your open position, closed position, overall profit — are losses coming inside your profit, or eating into your principal?"* (046 · 01:00:07)

Sizing/targets must adapt to the running book (T7). Corroborates T45 (against mechanical stops).

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T362** | On average, every third trade adds to capital | **Yes** — a KPI |
| **T363** | 0/50/100 are main decision lines; 25/75 are sub-boundaries | **Partly** — "70–90% follow" is a claim |
| **T364** | A completed leg of duration D retraces to its 50% within D/3 to 1.5·D | **Yes** |
| **T365** | Pullback ≤25% = structure (3 mo–1 yr), ≤50% = correction (1–3 yr), >50% = crash (5–7 yr; bottom within 1.5× the rise's candle count) | **Partly** — "trading" periods' chart unclear |
| **T366** | Greed trend: pullbacks stop at prior body/wick (~25%) → re-entry; fear trend: consecutive lower lows, no green | **Yes** |
| **T367** | After a control change price reaches at least the 50% of the current range before resting | **Yes** |
| **T368** | Level test outcomes: short of it / touch / break-and-return | **Yes** — scoring convention |
| **T369** | Intraday: 5m/15m/1H (+1m for NIFTY); monthly expiry: 1H/4H/D; investment: D/W/M | **Yes** — parameter choice |
| **T370** | 1H: candle 3 h, pattern 1–3 days, trend 1–3 weeks (conflicts with T345) | **Yes** — but pick one ladder |
| **T371** | Never a fixed 1:2 RR; targets depend on book P&L | **Partly** — no replacement formula here |

## Video 047 — Trend line + price line + volume: the under-value checklist and the "three ways" at a level

BTC is down 52% from its lifetime high, which he uses to separate correction (≤50%) from crash (>50%) and to name the zones (trade zone above 50%, investment zone below). He teaches how to draw a trend line (rotate from the extreme until it touches two bodies or two wicks), says price must be beyond **two** trend lines **and** the 50% of **two** price lines to count as under/over value, and adds a volume check (buy, sell, delta). He then describes the accumulation "climax", gap filling, and the rule that a level is met in three ways in no fixed order.

### T372 — ABOVE 50% = TRADE ZONE / OVER VALUE; BELOW 50% = INVESTMENT ZONE / UNDER VALUE; >50% FALL = CRASH

*"BTC has come down 52% from its lifetime high … 50% is a correction; it always happens. Only if it goes beyond 50% — 60, 70, 80 — is it called a crash. The zone above 50% is over value, below 50% under value; in trade terminology, above 50% is called the trade zone, and only below 50% is the investment zone."* (047 · 00:00:30 – 00:01:32)

Names the two halves of any range; the crash threshold matches T365.

### T373 — DRAWING A TREND LINE: ROTATE FROM THE EXTREME UNTIL IT TOUCHES ≥2 BODIES OR ≥2 WICKS

*"First place a straight line; the maximum high is here; slowly pull it toward that high. Where it hits more than one body or more than one wick — either of the two — stop. Both are correct methods."* (047 · 00:03:03)
*"Push the trend line straight toward this high; wherever it first hits — don't look before or after this high; between this high and this low, wherever it hits in the middle, stop there."* (047 · 00:10:40)
*"Connecting the two legs of the V pattern is the trend line."* (047 · 00:03:34)

A mechanical drawing rule that refines T14: anchor at the visible low (or high), pivot toward the opposite extreme, stop at the first contact with two bodies (or two wicks) inside that swing. Bodies or wicks — pick one and be consistent.

### T374 — UNDER / OVER VALUE NEEDS BOTH TREND LINES AND BOTH PRICE-LINE 50%s

*"You must draw two trend lines and look. Don't draw a single trend line and think it's in under value … it must be outside both. Then only under value; above both, then only over value."* (047 · 00:10:10 – 00:11:40)
*"Below the 50% of two price lines and below both trend lines — then the market has come to under value."* (047 · 00:12:42 – 00:13:12)
*"Just breaking the trend line and peeping out doesn't mean it has come to under value. It must break the price line's 50% too."* (047 · 00:18:17)

Two trend lines = the V's line and the A's line; two price lines = the 50% of the seller's leg and of the buyer's leg. All four must be on the same side. Tightens T38.

### T375 — ENTRY CHECK AT UNDER VALUE: BUY VOLUME OR DELTA MUST RISE

*"Volume has three things: buy volume, sell volume and delta volume — the difference between buy and sell at that particular price."* (047 · 00:17:17)
*"Price must be lower and volume higher. If price is low and only sell volume keeps increasing, it is still in the seller's control. Even if sell volume is high, how much delta is there — if delta is high, buyers are slowly accumulating."* (047 · 00:18:47)
*"Third, volume must be fulfilled — delta volume or buy volume. Even if that particular candle is red, if the delta volume is high you can buy."* (047 · 00:22:52 – 00:23:22)

Operational form of T68/T94 "buyer involvement": at the under-value zone (T374), require rising buy volume or a buyer-favourable delta, even on a red candle. He does not give a threshold *(unclear)*.

### T376 — THE ACCUMULATION CLIMAX: THE BIGGEST CANDLES COME AS PRICE BREAKS THE TREND LINE TOWARD THE PRICE LINE

*"Look at the candle sizes here — small, small, small. After breaking this, one aggressive candle, then candles two or three times that size. The speed comes where it breaks the trend line and where you think there is support."* (047 · 00:20:50)
*"Once delta rises, does it just go? No — that's where the climax is; it goes very aggressively down; don't be afraid … it will put brute candles bigger than any previous candle."* (047 · 00:23:22 – 00:24:23)
*"When does the climax happen? At the place where it breaks the trend line and is about to touch the price line."* (047 · 00:24:23)

Expect the largest candles of the swing between the trend-line break and the price-line (50%) touch; that is where the ladder's deeper legs fill (T51/T53). Also: a candle as large as the zone's largest candle marks control of greed/fear (047 · 00:36:04).

### T377 — THREE WAYS AT A LEVEL, IN NO FIXED ORDER; "TOUCH" MEANS THE BODY

*"Three things happen in the market: it turns without touching what you think is support; it touches and turns; it breaks and turns. The rich man keeps alternating these three flavours; that's where you get confused."* (047 · 00:21:20 – 00:22:22)
*"Sometimes it breaks on the first time; sometimes first no-touch, second touch, third touch, fourth break. Maximum three to four — up to five times the market has performed at the same boundary."* (047 · 00:31:29)
*"Touch means the body — don't take a wick touching as touching. The lower wick always belongs to the buyer."* (047 · 00:47:49 – 00:48:21)

Corroborates T368 and adds: a level can be tested up to 3–5 times; a "touch" is scored by body, not wick (T100).

### T378 — SHRINKING REBOUNDS FROM A BOUNDARY → IT BREAKS TO THE BASE

*"When it turned without touching it went this far; touched and turned — this far; broke and turned — only this far. So the buyer is getting weak … they will break this boundary and come again to this boundary's base."* (047 · 00:48:21 – 00:49:22)

Same logic as T340/T91 applied to the three test types.

### T379 — TREND LINE AND PRICE LINE ALTERNATE AS TARGETS; GAPS ARE FILLED BY BODIES

*"If it breaks the trend line it will come to the price line; breaking the price line it goes to the trend line. This is what happens in the market."* (047 · 00:35:03)
*"The market is first attracted to the trend, then to the price … if it has gone far, back to the trend line."* (047 · 00:14:42)
*"Is there a gap in this area? This gap will be filled again by body — not by wick … this is what they teach as fair value gap."* (047 · 00:30:28)

Target sequencing: after a trend-line break, next target = the price line's 50%; after that, the next trend line; unfilled body space ("gap") is revisited by bodies, however long it takes. Corroborates T73.

### T380 — SUB-BOUNDARIES ONLY ON LONG-TERM CHARTS

*"On 5 minute, 15 minute, 1 hour, don't draw sub-boundaries; draw sub-boundaries when it's long-term like this. Then it works clearly."* (047 · 00:54:00) *(heard once)*

Limits the 25/75 lines (T363) to D/W/M charts.

### T381 — ~7 IN 10 TIMES THE MARKET MOVES AGAINST THE RETAIL MINDSET

*"Only three times in ten will they act the market according to the retailer's mindset; seven times in ten the market operates against whatever the retailer thinks today."* (047 · 01:07:46 – 01:08:16) *(heard once)*

Not measurable without a definition of "retail mindset".

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T372** | Above 50% of range = over value / trade zone; below = under value / investment zone; fall >50% from high = crash | **Yes** — definition |
| **T373** | Trend line: pivot from the extreme toward the opposite extreme; stop at first contact with ≥2 bodies (or ≥2 wicks) within that swing | **Yes** |
| **T374** | Under value only when below both trend lines (V and A) AND below the 50% of both legs' price lines | **Yes** |
| **T375** | At under value, enter only with rising buy volume or buyer-positive delta (even on a red candle) | **Partly** — no threshold; needs order-flow data |
| **T376** | Largest candles of the swing occur between trend-line break and price-line touch | **Yes** |
| **T377** | A level resolves as no-touch / body-touch / break-and-return, in any order, up to 3–5 tests | **Yes** — scoring convention |
| **T378** | Successively smaller rebounds from the same boundary → it breaks to the base | **Yes** |
| **T379** | Trend-line break → target price-line 50%; body gaps get refilled | **Yes** |
| **T380** | 25/75 sub-boundaries only on D/W/M, not intraday | **Yes** — parameter |
| **T381** | ~70% of the time the market goes against the retail view | **No** — undefined |

## Video 048 — Reading one BTC level across Day / 4H / 1H: failed candles, the 50%-of-prior-candle test, contraction, and where the big money gathers

He returns to the BTC day-chart level from 047, then drills into it on 4H and 1H to show a failed candle, a confirmation window, and the buyer retaking control by closing beyond 50% of the prior candle. He shows a shrinking triangle of trend lines and says it breaks within one to three candles. He then stacks the Day/Week/Month 50% lines as the strong level and assigns horizons to the quarters (micro-short, short, mid, long), with the 75–100 quarter as where FIIs converge.

### T382 — WHERE THE BIG CANDLE PRINTS TELLS YOU CONTINUATION vs SETTLEMENT

*"To go further, it must put a candle as big as the biggest candle in this area … if a market must reach a place, it must travel fast."* (048 · 00:05:38 – 00:06:09)
*"At a support breakout or trend line, if a big candle goes, it means it's going aggressively. But if, before it comes near a support, it's already putting big, big candles, it means the market is going to settle there. These are symptoms; confirm with candle analysis."* (048 · 00:06:40)

A size test: a candle ≥ the zone's largest candle at a breakout = continuation; a run of large candles *before* arriving at a support = exhaustion/settlement at it. Corroborates T376.

### T383 — FAILED CANDLE NEEDS NEXT-CANDLE CONFIRMATION; WINDOW = 3 CANDLES

*"This body is a failed candle. What is a failed candle? A candle that puts a body without being able to break the previous candle's high or low — or, at a price line, not 50% of the body beyond and 50% below. For a failed candle, the next candle must confirm by putting a body below this candle's low: 'I'm going down; we sellers are strong' — the seller must prove it."* (048 · 00:10:15 – 00:11:17)
*"This is 50% outside candle. If they're going down, the next candle must confirm — within the next three hours. If this is one hour, confirm within the next three hours."* (048 · 00:15:22)

Adds a timing rule to T20/T355: a break candle that is failed (or merely 50% outside) must be confirmed by a body close beyond its extreme within 3 bars of the same timeframe. Note the failed-candle definition here ("cannot break the previous high/low") is looser than T355's 75/25 body split *(two definitions)*.

### T384 — CLOSE BEYOND 50% OF THE PRIOR CANDLE = CONTROL CHANGES HANDS

*"The next candle … closed beyond the previous candle's 50%. That is very important … within the next one hour they closed above 50% of the candle the seller brought — so the buyer has taken control."* (048 · 00:15:22 – 00:15:53)
*"Within the next hour, only if this current candle closes outside beyond the previous candle's 50% body will we escape."* (048 · 00:25:06 – 00:25:38)
*"If today the seller's candle closes below this 50%, it will come to this base; if the buyer maintains above this 50%, it can break the head and go higher."* (048 · 00:50:36 – 00:51:06)

A two-candle rule: the current candle closing beyond the midpoint of the previous candle's body flips control to its side. **May conflict with T25** ("~30% opposing force turns a red candle; at ~50% the move continues") — reconcile which side of 50% signals the turn.

### T385 — CONTRACTING TREND LINES BREAK TO THE LINE'S SOURCE IN 1–3 CANDLES

*"This boundary keeps shrinking — smaller than this, smaller than this … When the market reaches the state of hitting the trend lines up and down, it will blast one way — to the top of this area, the trend line's source above, or its source below — in a single candle if needed, at least one candle and at most within the next three candles."* (048 · 00:26:43 – 00:27:13)

Triangle rule: once successive swings between the two trend lines keep shrinking, the break travels to the trend line's origin (its first anchor) within 1–3 bars. Corroborates T104/T353 with a time bound.

### T386 — WHERE THE DAY, WEEK AND MONTH 50% LINES COINCIDE IS THE STRONG LEVEL

*"Weekly's 50% is right here … monthly's 50% is here … weekly, monthly, daily — all their 50% are in this place. So first the market will at least come to this area and take fuel, then come down again."* (048 · 00:32:15 – 00:33:17)
*"This place is a strong place; breaking it is no ordinary thing. Only if it breaks can the market go — and if it breaks aggressively, it will surely come to the 75."* (048 · 00:35:19)
*"Here is the monthly 50%, the weekly and the daily — that's the reason I trust this place strongly."* (048 · 00:41:54)

Confluence rule for T336: overlapping 50% lines from several timeframes make a level that holds on first contact; a break carries to the next quarter (75%).

### T387 — THE QUARTERS AS HORIZONS; 75–100 IS WHERE FIIs CONVERGE

*"The 25 above this — micro short term; 25 to 50 — short term; 50 to 75 — mid term; the boundary going 75–100 — long term."* (048 · 00:39:52)
*"75 to 100 — what boundary? Investment zone … in the long term the big rich men's involvement is highest."* (048 · 00:43:58)
*"When everyone's floating loss is heavy here, the FIIs and big rich men who came separately all come strongly into one place — that place is this 75-to-100 boundary … they will certainly bring it back into its 25-to-50 boundary."* (048 · 00:47:03 – 00:48:04)

Maps T97's quarters to horizons (also said in 047 · 00:13:42) and predicts that a move reaching the 75–100 quarter of a range is pushed back to at least the 25–50 quarter. The direction of counting (from which end) follows the leg being measured.

### T388 — THE MARKET FOLLOWS THE HIGHER TIMEFRAME; BIG MONEY IS INFREQUENT

*"A 5-minute candle is like short term, 15 minute mid term, 1 hour long term. The market always follows the long term; the big rich follow the long-term price movements."* (048 · 00:22:04)
*"A big FII comes into a particular stock once in three months, once in six months — they don't come daily with sacks."* (048 · 00:22:35)
*"Between 5 minute and 15 minute he gives priority to 15 minute; between 15 and 1 hour, to 1 hour … a 5-minute fall goes at most to its long-term boundary, gives a correction, and only then breaks further."* (048 · 00:48:35 – 00:49:05)

When timeframes disagree, weight the higher one. Corroborates T48/T348.

*Also corroborates T96:* a single monthly layer "is still 70% in the seller's control" (048 · 00:40:24).

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T382** | Candle ≥ zone's largest at a breakout → continuation; large candles before reaching support → settlement there | **Yes** |
| **T383** | A failed / 50%-outside break must be confirmed by a body beyond its extreme within 3 bars | **Yes** — two failed-candle definitions to reconcile |
| **T384** | Current candle closing beyond the prior candle's body midpoint = control flip | **Yes** — check against T25 |
| **T385** | Shrinking swings between two trend lines → break to the line's origin within 1–3 bars | **Yes** |
| **T386** | Where D/W/M 50% lines overlap, first contact holds; a break carries to 75% | **Yes** |
| **T387** | Quarter 75–100 of a range = investment/long-term zone; price pushed back to at least 25–50 from there | **Yes** |
| **T388** | On conflict, follow the higher timeframe; a lower-TF fall stops at its long-term boundary first | **Partly** — "long-term boundary" loosely defined |

## Video 049 — BTC settles at the D/W/M 50%: the trend-line "deadline", the three-bar hold, partial close, and what futures/options are for

He confirms the prior call (price came to the body/wick zone), then reads the same level on 4H, 1H, 15m, 5m and 1m: seller probes that fail over many hours, a buyer takeover, and the point on the trend line that acts as the trend's "deadline". He gives a three-bar confirmation rule for any timeframe, a time bound for the 25%→50% move, and the size of a genuine support-break candle. A long middle section explains futures and options as hedging tools for asset holders and why options buyers rarely win.

### T389 — THE TREND LINE'S "DEADLINE" IS ITS LAST BODY CONTACT BEFORE THE EXTREME

*"From the visible high, place the trend line straight and pull it toward this low; wherever more than one body hits — in this zone — if it has crossed all of this zone, officially there is no chance of coming down."* (049 · 00:18:17)
*"Wherever the 1-hour trend line hits at most — that place is the deadline for this trend. This is a very important thing; I'm only telling you this now for the first time."* (049 · 00:18:49 – 00:19:21)
*"Don't look after the low; before the low, where did it last hit … this area. If it crosses this area's body-wick, it will go here 100%."* (049 · 00:19:51 – 00:20:21)

New definition: the invalidation level of a trend is the body/wick of the last candle the trend line touched *before* the swing extreme; a close beyond it confirms the reversal toward the range's 50% (T379). Builds on T373.

### T390 — THREE-BAR HOLD: CLOSE BEYOND THE LAST CANDLE'S 50%, THEN NEVER A BODY BACK THROUGH IT

*"If it puts a body outside, don't believe at once that's it — we must give it three hours."* (049 · 00:02:33)
*"This last candle — it must close outside beyond its 50% … and again, a body must never come back beyond this 50% line — within the next three bars, whatever the candle: 5 minute, within 15 minutes; 1 hour, within three hours; day, within three days; week, within three weeks; month, within three months. If it closes above the last candle's 50% and after that never puts a body below that 50% line, confirm the market will go up again. This is the market settling."* (049 · 00:27:25 – 00:28:58)
*"Before finding the buy trend hours later by breaking the trend line, you can find it from the current candle itself. That is candle analysis."* (049 · 00:28:58)

Generalises T383/T384 into one testable rule on any timeframe: within 3 bars, a close beyond the reference candle's midpoint, and no subsequent body close back through it.

### T391 — ONCE THE 25% BREAKS, THE 50% COMES IN ⅓ OF THE LEG'S CANDLES

*"If the market surely breaks this 25, it will go aggressively to 50% — whatever number of candles it took coming from here, to go back to 50 it will take one-third of those candles. If it came in 100 candles, in the next 30 candles it will go to 50%. One-third of the time — this is the rich man's secret."* (049 · 00:32:00 – 00:32:30)

Sharpens T364: the ⅓·D lower bound is the expected time once the 25% retrace line is broken.

### T392 — A REAL SUPPORT BREAK PRINTS A CANDLE 1.5–2× THE ZONE'S LARGEST

*"If the sellers cross where they first brought it, it's very dangerous — the next boundary; the sellers become terribly aggressive — they call it breaking support; if they break support it goes terribly. Otherwise a big candle for a trap — at least a big candle: whichever is the biggest red candle in this area, it will put one and a half times, two times that."* (049 · 00:38:03 – 00:38:34)

Quantifies T382: a support break (or trap) candle is 1.5–2× the largest opposite-colour candle in the zone.

### T393 — PARTIAL CLOSE ("HAIRCUT") AT THE FIRST 50% TARGET

*"If it goes, it will come aggressively up to the 1-hour's 50% line. When it comes, those who did fund management clearly … will start reducing their positions — partial close, what they technically call a haircut."* (049 · 00:16:16 – 00:16:46)
*"If we have 10 quantity, here we can give away 5 quantity because overall profit has come; if it goes further, fine; if it comes down, we can do the mechanism."* (049 · 00:17:17)

Corroborates T31/T61 with a concrete split: about half the position at the first 50% target.

### T394 — FLAT BUYER LINE + FALLING SELLER LINE = DOWNTREND; BREAKING THE FLAT LINE → 50%

*"Connect these two A patterns fully — it's flat. But connect these, it's sloping. So the market is falling; the seller's power is higher; the buyer's is fairly flat … if they bring it down beyond this flat line, the buyer's boundary, they'll come to this 50%."* (049 · 00:55:24 – 00:55:54)

Descending-triangle reading using the two trend lines of T374: the flatter side is the weaker; a close through it targets the range's 50%.

### T395 — OPTIONS BUYERS RARELY WIN; TRADE OPTIONS ONLY AS A SELLER WITH ₹20–25 LAKH+

*"That's why option sellers earn more; the option buyer's chance of earning is very low … With 2,000, 3,000, 5,000, 10,000 you can never win in options. Knowledgeable people will say come near options only with 25 lakh, 50 lakh."* (049 · 00:45:11 – 00:45:41)
*"If you do options, only with 20–25 lakh can you do the mechanism and earn overall profit."* (049 · 00:51:49)

Corroborates T47 (futures/options exist to protect an asset: 049 · 00:42:08 – 00:44:40) and T79/T40. *Also:* "four tries over many hours without a body beyond the wick → ~90–99% it won't break" (049 · 00:13:43) corroborates T340/T347.

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T389** | Trend invalidation = body/wick of the last candle the trend line touched before the extreme; a close beyond it → move to the range 50% | **Yes** |
| **T390** | Within 3 bars, close beyond the last candle's 50% and no later body back through it = confirmed | **Yes** |
| **T391** | After the 25% retrace line breaks, the 50% is reached within ⅓ of the leg's bar count | **Yes** |
| **T392** | A genuine support break (or trap) prints a candle 1.5–2× the zone's largest opposite candle | **Yes** |
| **T393** | Close ~50% of quantity at the first 50% target; manage the rest | **Yes** |
| **T394** | Flat buyer line + falling seller line; close through the flat line → range 50% | **Yes** |
| **T395** | Don't buy options with small capital; options only as seller with ₹20–25 lakh+ | **Partly** — a capital policy |

## Video 050 — "Officially" a buy trend: the fortress level, repeating the largest candle, and trend line ↔ price line

He checks yesterday's BTC 1H call (price reached the marked trend-line contact and the 25% line, then turned), and explains why that contact was the level that "officially" changes the trend: it is where the last aggressive seller move began (the "fortress"). He shows that a move's largest candle being repeated marks full force / end of accumulation, restates that price alternates between trend line and price line, and gives the 5-minute confirmation sequence. About a third is on mindset, company, criticism and his background.

### T396 — A TREND CHANGES "OFFICIALLY" ONLY ON AN OUTSIDE CLOSE BEYOND WHERE THE LAST AGGRESSIVE MOVE BEGAN

*"Mark only the two bodies; wicks are never needed, because we sat it on more than one body."* (050 · 00:06:35)
*"Only if it crosses this place can the market go up — I said 'officially' it will go to buy. Only if it crosses here can it officially go up; otherwise the market will settle or correct … or break this low and go down."* (050 · 00:07:05)
*"If it put an outside candle beyond that, officially the market is a buy trend — the whole trend changes to buy: candle changes, pattern changes, the trend goes to the buy side."* (050 · 00:09:38 – 00:10:09)
*"From which place did the seller last start aggressively? … The market must go back beyond that same place. Where you lost, you must win — that is victory … planting the flag at that border."* (050 · 00:29:03 – 00:29:34)

Same level as T389, explained: the start of the last impulsive leg (the last trend-line body contact before the extreme). Before an outside close beyond it, treat moves as correction/chop. He confirms the trend line is drawn on ≥2 **bodies**, wicks ignored (refines T373).

### T397 — REPEATING THE MOVE'S LARGEST CANDLE = FULL FORCE; THEN CONTINUATION OR SETTLEMENT

*"How to see that accumulation is complete: in the falling market, whatever the biggest candle so far was, they will put one like it again there. If the market came from ₹100 to ₹80 and is going below 80, they confirm by putting the same size — or bigger — red candle as the biggest red candle between 100 and 80."* (050 · 00:10:40 – 00:11:43)
*"This is the full-force candle in this area — the biggest from this low to this high. Showing one more like it is like a signal … either they go down even more terribly, or they settle there — one of two. Read it in the next candles: after it, it must not put an outside candle beyond that candle's 50% or body-wick. If that happens, the market will come at least to the 50% of that high-low."* (050 · 00:22:57 – 00:23:57)
*"If it put the biggest green candle of the area and still can't break the body-wick above, it will certainly come down — they've used their full force."* (050 · 00:21:56 – 00:22:26)

A size-matching signal that refines T382/T392: a candle ≥ the leg's largest candle marks exhaustion of one side's force; the next candles decide (hold beyond its 50% → continuation; lose it → at least the range 50%).

### T398 — SETTLEMENT HAPPENS AT EITHER THE TREND LINE OR THE PRICE LINE

*"Breaking the trend line — price line; breaking the price line — trend line. The market settles in one of these two places."* (050 · 00:53:05)
*"Coming from above, it broke the 25; below that by price it's the 50, but it touched the trend line and turned. So if you thought it would come to 50, it won't — see where it settles. When it touches the trend line, downgrade the 1 hour to 15, 5, 1 minute and do candle analysis there."* (050 · 00:53:35 – 00:54:35)
*"Above both the trend line from above and the one from below — the market has begun to go to over value … don't buy new; exit the buy partially or fully at the higher price."* (050 · 00:52:02 – 00:53:05)

Corroborates T379 and T374; adds that whichever of trend line / price line comes first is the candidate settlement, confirmed on lower-timeframe candles.

### T399 — 5-MINUTE CONFIRMATION SEQUENCE

*"A clear outside candle — a red candle that doesn't touch this place, this candle. The rest are all failed candles."* (050 · 00:57:06 – 00:57:37)
*"If big bodies keep coming, each beyond the previous big body, the market has settled … Then wait three 5-minute candles: it must go above its 50% and must not come back below that 50% … a wick may come; if not even a wick comes into this area, even better — the buyer has come strongly."* (050 · 00:57:37 – 00:58:38)

Worked example of T390 on 5m. The "clear outside candle" wording again matches T355 (no overlap with the prior candle), not T95.

*Heard once, unclear:* the price that climbed along a trend line will eventually fall back through that line's starting point (050 · 00:49:00 – 00:49:31).

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T396** | Trend flips only on an outside close beyond the start of the last impulsive leg (last ≥2-body trend-line contact before the extreme) | **Yes** |
| **T397** | A candle ≥ the leg's largest candle = full force; hold beyond its 50% → continuation, else ≥ range 50% | **Yes** |
| **T398** | After a trend-line or price-line break, the first of the other line reached is where price settles | **Yes** |
| **T399** | 5m: clear outside candle, then within 3 bars close beyond its 50% with no body back through | **Yes** |

## Video 051 — Market "wavelength": channel shapes, parallel channels, and the "runway" extension boundary

He confirms the BTC 1H read from 050 (price stopped at the 25% line and re-entered), then says a market keeps the same swing "wavelength" for two or three patterns. He classifies the shapes two trend lines can make (equal, rising, falling; one side flat and the other expanding or contracting; both expanding or contracting) and what each implies. He adds parallel channels and a "runway mechanism" — a channel over a swing's high–low whose outer bands mark how far a breakout can extend before a reversal is due. He closes on time investment versus money and on quitting if you cannot learn it properly.

### T400 — THE SAME SWING "WAVELENGTH" REPEATS FOR 2–3 PATTERNS

*"Whatever model it went in — the same wavelength it had at the start — the market will follow that same wavelength two or three times … whatever sentiment there is, the market follows that sentiment for the next two or three patterns. That's why yesterday I drew that it would hit right here."* (051 · 00:05:06 – 00:05:37)

Projection rule: the next swing's size/angle ≈ the previous one's for 2–3 more swings. His own example is described, not measured *(heard once)*.

### T401 — A 25% BREAK THAT RE-ENTERS WITHIN 3 BARS HAS FAILED

*"It came beyond this 25 and put an outside candle; now, within the next three hours, it's again trying to put it inside. Only if it breaks this 25 price line and the trend line and puts an outside can it come to this 50% line. Otherwise the market will go up."* (051 · 00:06:07 – 00:06:37)

Applies T390's 3-bar window to the 25% line: to target 50% needs both the 25% line and the trend line broken and held.

### T402 — CHANNEL SHAPES: EQUAL / EXPANDING / CONTRACTING, AND WHAT EACH MEANS

*"A market's wavelength is only three kinds: equal; upper side; or down side — same wavelength."* (051 · 00:07:07 – 00:08:07)
*"Equal below and expanding above; equal above and expanding below; or expanding both above and below … and the opposite: it contracts too."* (051 · 00:10:09 – 00:11:41)
*"If it came from above expanding, the market is going to start contracting. If it contracts, it will blast one way … If it's contracting on the downside, get ready to buy; if it climbs and contracts on the upside, get ready to sell — the market has gone to over value. Coming from above expanding and contracting at the bottom means it has come to under value."* (051 · 00:18:18 – 00:19:49)
*"If the lower one is expanding, the market must be in a sell trend; if the upper one is contracting, it's becoming a sell trend; equal means neutral; the upper rising means up-trending."* (051 · 00:26:26 – 00:27:27)

A classification of the two trend lines of T374 (drawn per T373/T396). The trading implication: expansion → expect contraction next; contraction near the lows = buy-preparation zone, near the highs = sell-preparation zone; then a blast (T353/T385).

### T403 — PARALLEL CHANNEL: FAIL ON ONE SIDE → REACH THE OTHER

*"If it can't touch the top on one side and comes down, it will surely touch the lower one … one, two, three — unable to touch or break the lower, it will break the upper and go solidly up."* (051 · 00:22:52 – 00:23:24)
*"If it can't break below, it will at least go here — it will surely touch this. The nature of the trend line: if it can't break the lower, it will break the upper trend line, whether drawn single or as a parallel channel."* (051 · 00:28:58 – 00:29:28)

Parallel channel = the trend line copied to the opposite extreme of the same swing (051 · 00:21:51). Target after repeated failure at one side = the opposite side (or the range 50%, whichever first).

### T404 — "RUNWAY" EXTENSION BOUNDARY: A BREAKOUT CORRECTS INSIDE THE LONG-TERM BAND

*"This is the market's extension boundary … when a support or breakout breaks, how far can I wait — how long, how far?"* (051 · 00:29:59, 00:31:00 – 00:31:30)
*"We combine price and trend line — the channel for this high and this low; we call it the runway mechanism … Take the parallel channel, join it at the high and the low, bring it to where it hits between them, then draw its 50% line."* (051 · 00:32:01 – 00:33:01)
*"It shows dots: one-two-three — that's the short-term zone; the next three — mid-term zone; the last three — long-term zone."* (051 · 00:33:34 – 00:34:05)
*"If the seller doesn't bring it inside these last lines, the market won't come back — it goes into the buyer's control … If it doesn't correct within these three, 99.99% — if you have a sell position beyond that, throw it away and do the next work; the market will go to the next boundary."* (051 · 00:36:08, 00:40:43 – 00:41:14)

A channel drawn from the swing's high to low (both edges anchored on bodies/wicks), subdivided into short/mid/long bands; a breakout's counter-move is expected inside the long-term band, and a close beyond it invalidates the counter-trade. The band levels are *(unclear)* — he reads them off a charting tool's "dots" (apparently a Fibonacci channel), so they must be specified before testing.

### T405 — ~7 OF 10 SETUPS WORK; THE 3 FAILURES BREAK BRUTALLY

*"Seven times out of ten this will work. Three times it breaks — and those three times it breaks brutally; even your stop loss won't fill, it goes that fast. That's the big danger."* (051 · 00:43:19 – 00:43:49) *(heard once)*

The failure mode is gap-like, which is his argument for managing losers by mechanism (T11/T45) rather than stops.

### T406 — REPAIR AN INTRADAY LOSS ON A LONGER HORIZON ("TIME INVESTMENT")

*"If there's an intraday loss, don't think of fixing it again intraday — at least try in a weekly expiry to fix that loss. Money investment alone isn't enough; you must also put time investment."* (051 · 00:57:44)

Corroborates T48 (escalate to a higher timeframe with less money).

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T400** | Next 2–3 swings repeat the prior swing's size/angle | **Partly** — "wavelength" not quantified |
| **T401** | A close beyond the 25% line that re-enters within 3 bars = failed; the 50% needs 25% line + trend line broken | **Yes** |
| **T402** | Trend-line pair: expansion → contraction next; contraction at lows = buy zone, at highs = sell zone; then breakout | **Yes** |
| **T403** | Repeated failure at one channel edge → price reaches the opposite edge | **Yes** |
| **T404** | Breakout extension should correct inside the channel's long-term band; beyond it, abandon the counter-trade | **Partly** — band levels unspecified |
| **T405** | ~70% of setups work; failures are fast and gap-like | **Partly** — a claimed hit-rate |
| **T406** | Repair an intraday loss over a weekly-expiry horizon, not intraday | **Partly** — a management policy |

## Video 052 — Depth and structure in sequence: 50% / 25% pullbacks, the fast run that ends a leg, and the "inside A" pattern

BTC reached the predicted source zone; he then describes the *sequence* of pullbacks inside a trend: the counter-moves alternate between reaching about 50% and about 25% of the leg, and a pullback that cannot even make 25% precedes an acceleration. A run of 4–5 same-colour candles is a sign the move will soon settle, and he shows what the settlement looks like (a wick-only new low, then A-heads broken upward). He adds the "inside A pattern" and explains why a fixed 1:2 fails in this rhythm. The last third is on retail vs business-owner mindset and his early years in a broker's office.

### T407 — PULLBACKS ALTERNATE ~50% AND ~25%; ONE THAT CAN'T MAKE 25% PRECEDES ACCELERATION

*"From above it goes to the overall 50% — sometimes 75, or even the base … then the depth reaches here; within its 25-50-75 it goes to about 50, again breaks the low and comes down, again can't make 50 and turns at about 25 … at the start 50, next 50 … to make retail believe the false image that the market will never come down."* (052 · 00:06:43 – 00:07:46)
*"After a point, within the overall depth, the structure only goes to 25; beyond that it can't. When it goes only 25, the market comes down very fast — that's control of fear. The buyer couldn't even take it 25."* (052 · 00:08:17 – 00:08:48)
*"First it goes 25 even, can't make 50, comes fast, again goes 50, comes fast, again only 25 … it can go any way; the order isn't fixed."* (052 · 00:27:36 – 00:28:06)

Measured as a fraction of the most recent leg (depth, T354): pullbacks ≈50% are normal continuation; a pullback ≤25% signals the next leg will be fast. The order varies. Refines T97/T356.

### T408 — 4–5 SAME-COLOUR CANDLES IN A FAST RUN = SETTLEMENT SOON

*"Three candles, four, five candles continuously red — this happens mostly when it can't go even 25. If the market goes very fast up or down with four or five candles of the same colour, the market will settle soon — like a water lorry without brakes, soon it will crash into something — it's going to reverse."* (052 · 00:08:48 – 00:09:18)
*"Two or three times consistently red — a fast market will soon settle; over-speed is bad for the body."* (052 · 00:36:44)

An exhaustion count: after 4–5 consecutive same-colour candles (with ≤25% pullbacks), expect a settlement / reversal attempt. Relates to T102 (a side exhausting).

### T409 — WHAT THE SETTLEMENT LOOKS LIKE: A WICK-ONLY NEW LOW, THEN A-HEADS BROKEN

*"Then it comes fast, and as it turns back it doesn't break this low — it puts a wick beyond the low, no body — and slowly breaks head after head upward."* (052 · 00:09:18 – 00:09:48)
*"It couldn't break beyond this low and goes again; comes back without even touching this low — then the market has settled and gone into the buyer's control. Only then will the trend line from above come close and break, and the trend change to buy."* (052 · 00:22:32 – 00:23:03)

Bottom sequence: fast run (T408) → a new extreme by wick only (T100) → higher low that doesn't touch the prior low → A-heads broken → trend-line deadline (T396) broken.

### T410 — AFTER AN "OFFICIAL" BREAK, EXPECT A PULLBACK TO ITS 50% BEFORE CONTINUATION

*"It crossed officially — don't go and buy here. If he hits it, he'll surely come to its 50%, or break the base and go below … this pattern couldn't even go 25; it tried near 50; now don't decide 'officially buy' yet — it can even come to this source and go again."* (052 · 00:46:57 – 00:47:28)

Entry timing on top of T396: the official break is not the entry; the retrace to the break leg's 50% is.

### T411 — "INSIDE A PATTERN": A PULLBACK TO THE PRIOR A'S 50% = A BATTLE ZONE

*"It went up and came to its 50% — this is called an inside A pattern. Only beyond this is outside. Once it puts an inside pattern, a strong war is going on between buyer and seller in that area. For it to go further down, the A-pattern heads must come lower and lower."* (052 · 00:18:56 – 00:19:57)
*"Already came to 50, again in the inside pattern came to 50 — now it couldn't even bring it to 25. Then it goes aggressively up."* (052 · 00:20:27 – 00:20:57)

Extends T105 (inside pattern) with the 50%/25% measure of T407.

*Also:* the market revisits a price it left "however many months" later (052 · 00:03:03) — corroborates T73. 1H is "the major junction of the short term and the minor junction of the long term" (052 · 00:17:25 – 00:17:55). A fixed 1:2 fails because the 50%/25% alternation makes half the targets unreachable (052 · 00:32:10 – 00:33:12) — corroborates T371.

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T407** | Pullbacks alternate ~50% / ~25% of the last leg; a pullback ≤25% precedes a fast leg | **Yes** |
| **T408** | 4–5 consecutive same-colour candles in a fast run → settlement/reversal attempt soon | **Yes** — "soon" undefined |
| **T409** | Bottom = fast run → wick-only new low → higher low not touching it → A-heads broken → trend-line break | **Yes** |
| **T410** | After an official trend-line break, wait for the retrace to the break leg's 50% | **Yes** |
| **T411** | A pullback to the prior A's 50% = inside pattern; continuation needs lower A-heads; a later pullback ≤25% → fast move the other way | **Yes** |


## Video 053 — Correction vs crash cycles, control of fear at main lines, the vertical-drop climax, and trend-line speed

He reads a long-term BTC chart. He separates a routine 50% correction from the all-time high from a crash beyond 50%, and gives a recurrence period in years for each. He also covers four things: where control of fear shows up (at the 0/50/100 lines, not 25/75); how an escalator decline turning vertical marks the climax; how holding the trend line shortens the time to the 50% target; and how he judges a forceless aggressive candle and the end of a buyer's push. He restates the 3-candle hold (00:04:06, corroborates T390). The tomato-seller volume analogy (00:52:50–00:55:56) adds nothing beyond T375.

### T412 — CORRECTION EVERY 3–5 YEARS, CRASH EVERY 5–7 YEARS

*"a 50% [fall] coming is just an ordinary event … from the all-time high, 50% very commonly, for every script, once in three to five years a 50% will definitely happen"* (053 · 00:06:08 / 00:06:38)
*"going past that 50% up to 75% we call a crash … up to 50% is a correction, it will definitely happen"* (053 · 00:06:38)
*"going past [50%], past 75, is the crash. Within 5 to 7 years it will definitely happen in most scripts"* (053 · 00:07:09)
*"if there is depth there must be structure … the market will create a structure of at least 25% of it and only then come down aggressively again"* (053 · 00:08:13)

This defines correction as a fall of up to 50% from the all-time high, and crash as a fall beyond 50% (toward and past 75%). It **conflicts with T365**, which gave 1–3 years for a correction; the coordinator must pick one. The 25% structure after a deep fall corroborates T97/T364. The recurrence is a market claim he did not test *(heard once in this form)*.

### T413 — CONTROL OF FEAR HAPPENS AT THE 0 / 50 / 100 LINES; 25 / 75 IS THE SMOOTH TRAP

*"how to find the market is in control of fear … it happens when it breaks a boundary — boundary means, at most, when it breaks this 50, or the 0 above and the 100 below"* (053 · 00:10:46)
*"when the market reaches the 25 above or the 75 below, the market shows smooth, as if it won't go down any more"* (053 · 00:10:46)
*"slowly inside that [micro] boundary it keeps circling patiently, making you believe you profit wherever you buy … then from there it crashes suddenly"* (053 · 00:11:16)

The panic moves happen on breaks of the main quarter lines (0/50/100). The 25/75 "micro boundaries" produce quiet grinding that lures buyers in before a sharp break. This refines T97.

### T414 — A VERTICAL DROP AFTER AN ESCALATOR DECLINE MEANS THE CLIMAX IS NEAR

*"until now it came in the escalator model, now it will fall vertically like rain falling"* (053 · 00:17:56)
*"the price has gone very far from the trend line and travels back toward it … when it falls like rain, you can think"* (053 · 00:18:27)
*"the market has moved toward the climax … it happens in every frame, 1 minute, 5 minute, monthly … if it falls vertically and goes near a boundary, touches it or breaks it"* (053 · 00:18:57)
*"at that place the climax is coming, the market is going to settle; there you can prepare for the next entry — under value, buy; over value, sell"* (053 · 00:19:27)

Before the vertical leg, he notes the fast leg cannot even retrace 25% (00:17:56). This connects T8 (escalator slopes) and T73 (distance from the trend line implies return) to an entry-readiness signal. "Vertical" is not quantified (no slope or distance threshold is given).

### T415 — TREND LINE HELD → FAST TO THE 50%; TREND LINE BROKEN → DELAYED

*"a wick may go past this line, but a body must not … and from this low it should not break this trend line"* (053 · 00:22:28)
*"whenever it goes without breaking the trend line, the time becomes very little to touch the 50% line above"* (053 · 00:22:28)
*"if it keeps breaking the trend line and coming back, it will be very delayed … a broken trend line means buyer–seller war is tight there … it comes to the price line, refuels there"* (053 · 00:22:59)

This is a timing claim: the same 50% target is reached in fewer bars when no body closes through the trend line. It corroborates T100 (wick allowed, body not) and complements T364's time window. It is testable as a comparison of time-to-50% by whether a trend-line body break occurs.

### T416 — AN AGGRESSIVE CANDLE THAT GIVES BACK ITS OWN 50% HAS NO FORCE

*"it went aggressively but there is no big force … before that same candle finished it came back below that candle's 50%"* (053 · 00:27:08)
*"only if the body goes past this wick, and even then for the next three hours the body [stays] above that 50%"* (053 · 00:27:08)
*"only if it maintains above, without coming below the 50% of the last candle whose body carried the force, is there a chance to go further up"* (053 · 00:27:41)

A single aggressive candle whose close is back beyond its own midpoint is not force. Continuation needs a later body past that candle's wick, then bodies holding above the force candle's 50%. The caption says "three **hours**" (மூணு மணி நேரம்). On the chart he was reading, that may equal three candles (T390); whether he meant hours or bars is *(unclear)*. Otherwise this corroborates T390/T20.

### T417 — BREAKING THE BUYER'S LAST BODY/WICK ENDS THE PUSH → 50% OF THE SOURCE

*"it must not come past the body-wick the buyer already carried; if it does, there is a chance to break this source"* (053 · 00:33:51)
*"a pusher goes in one force … takes it to a safe distance, and if it comes back down, comes to its 50%, gathers people and pushes further up"* (053 · 00:33:51)
*"when it breaks the body-wick already carried and comes down, that buyer's strength is over … the market will come aggressively down to the 50% from the source the buyer carried it from"* (053 · 00:34:22 / 00:34:53)

A healthy push retraces only to its own 50% to "refuel". A close through the buyer's last push candle (body and wick) means the push is exhausted, and the target becomes the 50% of the whole move from its source. This corroborates T101 and T410 with a concrete trigger.

### T418 — DO NOT JUMP ON A BREAK; CHECK THREE CANDLES ON SMALLER FRAMES

*"that is what we call a trap, it shows as if going up … the moment it breaks you must not panic; if it broke, go to the next smaller frames and see how each three, three, three candles perform — you must not jump in the moment it breaks"* (053 · 00:39:59)

This is the same 3-candle confirmation (T390), applied on the lower timeframes after a higher-timeframe break. It corroborates T1/T20.

### T419 — CHOOSE THE MICRO TIMEFRAME BY SESSION LENGTH

*"you don't need to look at 1 minute for bitcoin, because this market is 24 hours … looking at 5 minutes is enough … in a market that is only 8 hours, 10 hours, looking at 1 minute is enough, you can consider that short term"* (053 · 00:50:47)

For a 24-hour market, 5m is the micro/short-term frame. For an 8–10 hour session, 1m is. *(heard once)* This corroborates T369 and T74.

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T412** | From the ATH: a ≤50% fall (correction) recurs every 3–5 yrs; a >50% (toward 75%+) crash every 5–7 yrs; after a deep fall, a ≥25% structure forms before the next aggressive leg | **Partly** — recurrence needs decades of data per instrument; conflicts with T365 (1–3 yrs) |
| **T413** | Panic (control-of-fear) moves occur on breaks of 0/50/100; the 25/75 zones show smooth, low-volatility grinding before a sharp break | **Partly** — "smooth" and "panic" need a volatility measure |
| **T414** | An escalator decline turning into a vertical drop far from the trend line, reaching/touching/breaking a boundary → climax near; prepare the next entry | **Partly** — "vertical" is not quantified |
| **T415** | If no body closes through the trend line, the 50% target is reached in fewer bars than when the line is broken | **Yes** |
| **T416** | An aggressive candle closing back past its own 50% has no force; continuation needs a body past its wick and then bodies holding beyond that 50% for three bars/hours | **Partly** — hours vs bars *(unclear)* |
| **T417** | A close through the buyer's last push body/wick → price travels to 50% of the move from its source | **Yes** |
| **T418** | After a higher-timeframe break, require 3 confirming candles on the lower frames before acting | **Yes** |
| **T419** | Micro timeframe = 5m for 24h markets, 1m for 8–10h sessions | **No** — a timeframe choice, not a market claim |

## Video 054 — Boundaries, body-wick zones, "control of greed/fear", and the refuel rule (BTC, gold, NIFTY multi-timeframe walk-through)

A long top-down read of Bitcoin (day → 4H → 1H → 15m → 5m → 1m), then gold and NIFTY. He ranks the 50% line above the 25/75 lines, defines "control of greed / fear" as runs of 3+ same-colour candles whose pullbacks cannot reach 25%, formalises "previous-candle" vs "current-candle" body-wick analysis, and introduces the "fuel" idea: price touches the nearest 50% line on the other side before it can reach a distant one. The last ~35 minutes are course/mindset talk (skipped except two money rules).

### T420 — 50% IS THE MAJOR BOUNDARY; 25/75 ONLY ON BIG FRAMES

*"Since this is a day chart, the important boundary — 25 and 75 are a sub-boundary; the important boundary is only 50. But in a big frame the sub-boundary also works as somewhat important."* (054 · 00:01:33)
*"Only in the very long move, apart from this 50%, boundaries like 25, 75 will give the market some support … only in the big boundary; in small ones you need not take those 25/75."* (054 · 00:02:04)
*"The 50% boundary is where lots of volume is exchanged — whether the market proceeds further up or cannot go and comes down mostly happens at 50%."* (054 · 00:02:34)
*"The trendline of this area will always be near 25% — near the 25 price line. That is why I said these 25/75 lines are not needed; it is not a major boundary, it is a minor boundary."* (054 · 00:33:34)

Refines T97 (quarters): the quarters still measure bounces, but as *support/resistance levels* only the 50% counts on small frames; 25/75 are used only on day-scale / long-distance moves. Also at 00:21:23 he says once down on the 1H "just watching 50, 75 as the outline is enough".

### T421 — CONTROL OF GREED / FEAR = 3+ SAME-COLOUR CANDLES WITH PULLBACKS UNDER 25%

*"Control of greed — whether green candles went consistently more than three."* (054 · 00:08:38)
*"Control of fear when coming down, greed when going up. So what is [greed]? Continuously three being green."* (054 · 00:11:39–00:12:09)
*"It goes such that the market does not come even to the 25% of the whole of this low… it does not come even to 25, and goes up even more aggressively."* (054 · 00:12:40)
*"Control of fear: without giving a chance to the structure, without reversing even 25, the market falls very aggressively, consistently, vertically. If it falls vertically like that once or twice, only after that is there a chance for the market to settle. That vertical fall — that is the climax."* (054 · 00:15:43)

A named regime: ≥3 consecutive candles of one colour in one timeframe, and counter-moves that stay under 25% of the whole leg ("rocket" vs "escalator"). Extends T96 (3+ layers) by adding the <25% pullback condition, and states the exit expectation: after one or two such vertical legs the market settles.

### T422 — THE 3-CANDLE RUN ESCALATES THROUGH TIMEFRAMES

*"It goes like a rocket — it will put three, four green candles continuously in one frame; in 1 hour it puts them in 1 hour… the 1-hour control of greed is over; now the market comes down again, corrects, and when it goes again it will put more than three green candles in the 4 hours itself. This happens frame by frame."* (054 · 00:13:10)
*"In 5 minutes it has put three [red] consecutively; in 15 minutes it put only two. Now it will [correct], go some distance to the 50% of this area, take a break, and if it breaks again then next in 15 minutes three red must go consistently… when it breaks on the 1 hour, for depth to go further, on the 1 hour three, four candles go consistently red."* (054 · 00:32:04–00:32:34)

Sequence claim: run of 3 on TF n → correction to ~50% → run of 3 on TF n+1 (5m → 15m → 1H → 4H). Testable by counting runs per timeframe.

### T423 — SECOND FAILURE TO REACH THE OVERALL 50% = AGGRESSIVE MOVE THE OTHER WAY

*"They already went to 50; if the second time it comes back without going to 50 of this overall, it means the market next is going to go down aggressively."* (054 · 00:15:12)

*(heard once)*. A lower-high test measured against the overall range's 50%.

### T424 — A FAST MOVE CONTINUES ONLY PAST THE BASE OF ITS BIGGEST CANDLE; WICK-ONLY PROBES FOR 3–4 HOURS = THE LEVEL HOLDS

*"The biggest body-wick that came fast in this area is this candle — it must not go below this body-wick. … Only if it goes below that candle's base will it go down; otherwise the chance is low."* (054 · 00:16:43)
*"If it is really going down, it may even put a wick beyond this — it will still come down. But it must not put a body beyond this. If it puts a body, that's it: it goes aggressively up."* (054 · 00:18:15)
*"There is a low; at that low it puts only wicks beyond the body-wick, not a body — if that sustains three hours, four hours, then it is hard for the market to break that place."* (054 · 00:18:15–00:18:46)

Corroborates T100 (body owns, wick belongs to the other side) and adds a number: 3–4 hours of wick-only probes without a body close marks the level as holding.

### T425 — PREVIOUS-CANDLE vs CURRENT-CANDLE BODY-WICK ANALYSIS

*"By previous market analysis, sometimes the last is a wick, sometimes a body; we take the last body-wick, that's all… if both are body, two bodies; if both are wick, two wicks… and if this candle is green, taking where the previous red is — that is even better."* (054 · 00:35:04–00:35:35)
*"Previous-candle analysis means taking previous plus current together. Current candle means taking only this one candle — take the body-wick of just that one candle."* (054 · 00:36:06)
*"The candle is seeing even faster, immediately — to see how the market will be within the next three candles, if we do previous-candle and current-candle analysis we can understand to some extent."* (054 · 00:42:47)

Defines the reference zone: the last body line and the last extreme wick line (of the previous opposite-colour candle when the current is green/red). Price continuing requires a body close beyond both lines; the read is valid for ~3 candles. Links to T92 (a read expires in a few bars).

### T426 — "OFFICIAL" BREAK = BODY BEYOND BOTH THE BODY-WICK LINE AND THE TRENDLINE

*"First by candle it must break these two lines; likewise by trend, only if it breaks this will it go there… only if it hits [breaks] both these lines can the market officially become a buy… when does it put a body beyond this — only then officially buy. Otherwise the market will 100% come down."* (054 · 00:24:24–00:24:54)

Trendline construction at 00:24:24: *"Put it straight at the top, bring it toward wherever the last extreme low is, and stop where the trendline touches"* — the anchoring differs from T14 (anchored on heads) *(unclear which side he rotates from; shown on chart only)*.

### T427 — AFTER A TRENDLINE BREAK, NO RE-ENTRY WITHIN 3 CANDLES

*"It breaks the trendline and puts an outside candle. Now what must not happen: within the next three candles it must not go back inside the trendline. If it goes, it goes up [reverses]."* (054 · 00:43:47–00:44:18)

*(heard once)*. A failed-break filter with a fixed window of 3 candles.

### T428 — APPROACH SEQUENCE TO A LEVEL: MISS, TOUCH, BREAK

*"Before coming to that place, it goes back as if 'I will not break it'… it goes without touching it, touches and goes, then we break it and come fast consistently. The market clearly follows these three things."* (054 · 00:31:02)
*"This 50% — it goes without touching. Next it touches and goes. Next it breaks and goes. This is how the market must perform."* (054 · 00:44:18)

Claim: a level is broken on the third approach after a miss and a touch. Consistent with T15 (2–4 tests before a break).

### T429 — FAILURE AT CANDLE LEVEL SENDS PRICE TO THE OPPOSITE PATTERN; FAILURE AT PATTERN LEVEL, TO THE OPPOSITE TREND

*"If this current candle breaks the previous candle's body and closes below, the market will definitely come to this place — write it down… if the current candle cannot perform as the previous candle, it will definitely come to the previous opposite's pattern."* (054 · 00:45:18–00:45:48)
*"If even at candle level the buyer has no force, the market comes to the seller's pattern. Likewise if at pattern level the buyer has no force, it comes to the seller's trend level."* (054 · 00:45:48)

Target ladder candle → pattern → trend (T27 vocabulary). The candle-level trigger is a close beyond the previous candle's body.

### T430 — AN AGGRESSIVE BREAK = A CANDLE ≥2× THE LARGEST GREEN CANDLE IN THE RANGE

*"Aggressive means: whatever the big green candle is within this area to this area, it will put at least two times that. That is breaking aggressively. From that we can know the market has started control of greed."* (054 · 00:52:27)

*(heard once)*. A numeric break-quality test; compare T108 (1.5–2× the last counter-move) — here it is the candle size, not the leg.

### T431 — REFUEL RULE: TO REACH A FAR 50%, PRICE FIRST TOUCHES THE NEAREST 50% ON THE OTHER SIDE

*"If it must touch the upper 50% line, the fuel is in the lower 50% line… to touch the upper 50% line, the key is in the lower 50% line."* (054 · 00:54:58)
*"To touch the far 50%, it goes to the near 50% and puts fuel, fuel, fuel and comes. So if it comes without putting fuel, it means with the fuel already put the market is going to go far, fast."* (054 · 01:08:11)
*"Instead of continuing the travel beyond this, it turned back and put fuel. Now they will definitely go there… very, very low chance of putting fuel again in between; they go in one stretch."* (054 · 01:09:43–01:10:13)
*"If they cancel their journey, it must turn at this 50% and break this; only if it breaks and puts an outside [close] beyond — 'they have no idea of going up, they have turned the vehicle home' — base means where the buyer started."* (054 · 01:10:44–01:11:14)

The "near" 50% is the next-lower-timeframe (or nearer) range midpoint; the "far" one is the higher-timeframe midpoint. One refuel then a single stretch to the far target; invalidated only by an outside close back through the refuel 50%, which targets the move's origin.

### T432 — AFTER A DEEP FALL (≈50%), A ≥25% RETRACEMENT COMES BEFORE CONTINUATION

*"The market is already 52% down; again it must go at least to about 25% of it, put fuel, and only then come down — it cannot come in one stretch, without a break. If there is depth there will be structure."* (054 · 00:26:26)

(His BTC 37–40k forecast at 00:25:55–00:28:01 is a forecast, not a rule — omitted.)

### T433 — MONEY: INCOME AT 1/100 OF CAPITAL; NO MARGIN; 100 DEMO + 100 LIVE TRADES

*"If you have ₹10,000, erase two zeros — if you have income in those ₹100 [multiples]… otherwise your account is in danger; whenever it is, that 10,000 will go."* (054 · 01:33:42–01:34:12) *(unclear: per trade or per period, and whether "above" or "at" ₹100 is the danger — the caption is garbled)*
*"With margin you will get good percentages, but really, don't do it with margin — that is borrowing."* (054 · 01:30:08)
*"I have already said you must do 100 demo trades and 100 live trades."* (054 · 01:16:22)

Margin ban corroborates T46/T80. The 1/100 ratio fits T39/T63 but the unit is unclear.

Also corroborates: T73 (far from the trendline, price will move back to it — 00:04:35); T38 (above 50% of visible high-low = over value, below = under value, buy under value — 00:48:21–00:50:23); T105/T99 head logic (00:40:43: a head put barely below the prior head, without 50% of body below, is a "failed candle").

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T420** | On small frames only the 50% of the high-low is a level; 25/75 act as levels only on day-scale / long moves | **Partly** — "big frame" not defined in numbers |
| **T421** | ≥3 consecutive same-colour candles whose pullbacks stay <25% of the leg = greed/fear regime; after 1–2 such vertical legs the market settles | **Yes** |
| **T422** | A 3-candle run on TF n is followed by a ~50% correction and then a 3-candle run on TF n+1 | **Yes** |
| **T423** | A second failure to reach the overall 50% precedes an aggressive move the other way | **Yes** |
| **T424** | Price continues only on a body close beyond the base of the largest fast candle; 3–4 h of wick-only probes = the level holds | **Yes** |
| **T425** | Reference zone = last body line + last extreme wick (prior opposite-colour candle); continuation needs a body close beyond both; the read lasts ~3 candles | **Yes** |
| **T426** | Official buy = body close beyond both the body-wick line and the trendline | **Partly** — trendline anchoring unclear |
| **T427** | After an outside-candle trendline break, a return inside within 3 candles = failed break | **Yes** |
| **T428** | A level is typically missed, then touched, then broken | **Yes** |
| **T429** | Close beyond the previous candle's body → price reaches the opposite pattern's body-wick; failure at pattern level → opposite trend level | **Partly** — the target levels are shown, not defined |
| **T430** | Aggressive break = candle ≥2× the largest same-colour candle in the range | **Yes** |
| **T431** | Before reaching the far (higher-TF) 50%, price touches the near 50% on the other side; after one refuel it goes in one stretch; an outside close back through the refuel 50% targets the origin | **Yes** |
| **T432** | After a ~50% fall, expect a ≥25% retracement before continuation | **Yes** |
| **T433** | Expected income ≈ capital/100; no margin; 100 demo + 100 live trades first | **Partly** — the period/unit of the 1/100 is unclear |

## Video 055 — Stacked 50% lines as a zone, refuel logic across 1m/5m/15m/1H, and "weightage" (who holds the stock)

BTC 1H → 15m → 5m → 1m. He restates the three V types, shows that two lower-timeframe 50% lines on the same side form a zone rather than two targets, and gives the continuation-vs-reversal test at a 50% line. A long middle section explains "weightage": at over value stock passes from the rich (own money) to retail (margin), who cannot hold it, so price falls fast to the 50% and below. Much of 00:25–01:14 and 01:18 onward is social/mindset commentary (skipped).

### T434 — TWO SAME-SIDE 50% LINES = ONE ZONE; PRICE NEED NOT TOUCH BOTH

*"The 1-hour 50% line is below; the 5-minute and 15-minute 50% lines are above. So those are two lines — then it is a boundary. This is one line — it is single.
