So the market that goes up need not go to the 15-minute line and then the 5-minute line, both; it will go somewhere inside that boundary and come back."* (055 · 00:11:11)
*"Only if it breaks this can it go to the 100% line above, because the 100% line of all of them — 15 minute, 5 minute, 1 hour — is above."* (055 · 00:11:44)

Target rule: when the 50% lines of two timeframes sit on the same side, treat the span between them as the target zone; a close beyond the whole zone targets the common 100% (the range extreme). Refines T431 (the "near 50%").

### T435 — AT A 50% LINE: TURN = CONTINUATION ATTEMPT; OUTSIDE CLOSE THROUGH = REVERSAL ATTEMPT

*"If it cannot break beyond a 50% line and — touching, not touching, or poking through — goes back again, it means it is trying to go beyond the existing base."* (055 · 00:17:52)
*"If it touches and goes up, it has fuelled and goes up. If it touches, breaks and goes further down, putting an outside candle, it means it is trying the opposite — it did not value that place; lots of volume got exchanged."* (055 · 00:18:24)
*"Only if it comes up beyond this resting point is it confirmed that they are going to go."* (055 · 00:19:56)
*"Only if it breaks that 1-minute 50% and puts an outside candle outside will it go aggressively to touch the 1 hour."* (055 · 00:16:50)

Binary test at every 50% line: a rejection (any wick behaviour) means the prior direction resumes toward/beyond the old base; an outside close through it means the line failed and price travels the other way. The "resting point" is the intermediate (15m) 50%. Operationalises T101 at 50% lines.

### T436 — OFFICIAL TREND CHANGE = BREAK OF THE LAST TRENDLINE TOUCH POINT

*"If the trend is officially changing, it will go beyond this area, break the place where the trendline already touched, and only if it crosses that already-touched place will it officially become a buy trend. Otherwise until then the market will at most hit this 50% line and this trendline and turn."* (055 · 00:09:07–00:09:37)

Before that, a move to the 50% line or the trendline is a correction inside the old trend. The trendline is drawn between the V and the A heads (00:05:33).

### T437 — FROM OVER VALUE, THE FALL TO 50% NEEDS NO BIG PLAYER AND TAKES ~⅓ OF THE CANDLES OF THE RISE

*"However strongly it went up, however many times the candles went, within a third of the candles it will come down with a thud. Why? Whenever the market is in the retailer's hand, the weightage becomes more."* (055 · 00:34:46) *(heard once; "a third" is my reading of "மூணுல ஒரு பக்கம்")*
*"If the market is going up, to that 50% line — without the rich man's help at all, if he just sells and leaves — it will come to the 50% line on its own, swiftly. Only if he pushes again from there will it go up; otherwise it breaks and comes down and he will strongly base in the under value."* (055 · 00:55:11)
*"When it comes to under value … they will run the biggest climax below under value."* (055 · 00:54:09)
*"A person who takes ₹1 lakh and uses the ₹5 lakh margin … the same 10% fall takes ₹50,000 — 50% of the money put in is gone; which human can stay unafraid?"* (055 · 00:45:03–00:45:34)

Claim: a rise into over value (above the long-term 50%) is retraced to the 50% in about one third of the candles it took to rise, and the climax low is printed below the 50% (under value). Compare T107 (stages take ⅓ then ½ of the candles). The margin arithmetic corroborates T79/T80.

### T438 — TIMEFRAME SETS: 1m/5m/15m/1H FOR TRADING; 1H/4H/D/W/M FOR INVESTMENT; OVERALL TREND = SIDE OF THE STACKED 50% LINES

*"5 minute, 15 minute, 1 hour, 1 minute are for trade. Then 1 hour, 4 hour, day, week, month — this is for investment."* (055 · 01:14:58)
*"The 1-hour 50% is here, and the 5-minute and 15-minute ones are here; if the market is below all of these, it is in an overall downtrend."* (055 · 01:14:58–01:15:29)

A definition of overall trend: price below the 50% line of 5m, 15m and 1H = overall down (above all = overall up). Consistent with T38.

### T439 — THREE SUCCESSIVELY LOWER PUSHES NEED AN AGGRESSIVE BREAK OR PRICE FALLS

*"The buyer took it this far the first time, only this much the second time; the third time, even if it hits here, this high will be less than this high … only if it comes here and breaks aggressively will it go to that source; otherwise the market will break fast and come here."* (055 · 01:17:32)

Corroborates T91 (shrinking pushes = weakening buyer) with a three-push count.

Also corroborates: T22 (V types: Failed = does not reach the prior base, Equal = touches it, Extra = breaks beyond and returns — 00:02:31–00:03:01); T73 (far from the trendline → returns at least to it — 00:06:04); T431 (fuel = volume accumulated before a forceful move — 00:13:46).

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T434** | When two timeframes' 50% lines sit on the same side, the span between them is one target zone; a close beyond the zone targets the range's 100% | **Yes** |
| **T435** | At a 50% line: rejection → prior direction resumes toward the old base; outside close through → move the other way; confirmation needs a close beyond the intermediate (15m) 50% | **Yes** |
| **T436** | Trend changes officially only when price breaks the last trendline touch point; before that, 50%/trendline tests are corrections | **Partly** — the touch point is shown, not defined |
| **T437** | From over value, price returns to the long-term 50% in ~⅓ of the candles of the rise; the climax low forms below the 50% | **Yes** (count candles) — ratio heard once |
| **T438** | Trade on 1m/5m/15m/1H, invest on 1H–month; price below all of the 5m/15m/1H 50% lines = overall downtrend | **Yes** |
| **T439** | Three successively lower highs on a bounce → a fall unless the third breaks aggressively | **Yes** |

## Video 056 — Control percentages from body/wick overlap, depth vs structure, and time-boxed forecasts (4 h / 8 h / 24 h)

He reviews the previous day's BTC call, then scores who controls a retest by whether the new body/wick went past the old body/wick (50/50, 70/30, 100%). He defines depth (seller legs) and structure (buyer legs), says the third counter-move that fails to re-enter the prior leg starts the aggressive phase, and for the first time attaches time windows to his reads: what the 1m 50% must do within 4 hours, what follows within 8 hours, and what a low break means for the next 24 hours. Gold and NIFTY follow. Long closing section on persistence (skipped).

### T440 — CONTROL SCORE AT A RETEST: BODY-PAST-BODY, WICK-PAST-WICK

*"The lower wick belongs to the buyer, the upper wick belongs to the seller; the body belongs to the buyer if green and to the seller if red."* (056 · 00:08:14–00:08:45)
*"First there was a body, there was a wick; it put a body beyond the body, but did not put a body beyond the wick — that is a minus; body beyond body is a plus … officially it must put a body beyond this wick."* (056 · 00:11:15)
*"Here it put body beyond body but not body beyond wick — 50/50. Here it put only a wick beyond the wick and no red body beyond the body — 70/30: 70% the buyer's control, only 30% the seller's. Here neither body beyond body nor wick beyond wick — 100% the buyer's control; from now the market comes only inside this body-wick, and breaking this low is very, very hard — not within about three hours."* (056 · 00:11:46–00:12:16)

A graded score of the attacking side on each retest of a prior candle's body/wick zone: body past body only = 50/50; wick past wick only = 70/30 for the defender (he also says "80%" at 00:11:15 — *(unclear which)*); neither = 100% defender, level safe for ~3 hours. Only a body past the wick is a real takeover. Refines T100/T94.

### T441 — DEPTH vs STRUCTURE; THE THIRD COUNTER-MOVE THAT CANNOT RE-ENTER STARTS THE RUN

*"If depth keeps increasing, the market is in the seller's control; if structure keeps increasing, the market is more in the buyer's control."* (056 · 00:23:00)
*"First, within the distance the buyer took, the seller came; second, within this; third, without coming inside this high-to-low it goes up — only then does it go aggressively."* (056 · 00:24:03)
*"When it goes aggressively: it puts a candle bigger than any green candle so far; second, three or more green candles continuously … then it settles: beyond a certain place only the wick goes big, the body goes inside the wick but not beyond the wick … then a body comes back beyond the wick — it means the market has settled."* (056 · 00:24:34–00:25:04)

Depth = a seller leg that exceeds the previous seller leg; structure = the buyer leg. Counting rule: two pullbacks retrace into the previous push; the third that stays outside it starts the aggressive phase (T421/T430 markers), which ends when bodies stop following the wicks. Links T106/T108.

### T442 — CONTRACTION = PRICE BETWEEN THE LOWER-TF 50%s AND THE 1H 50%; THE 1H IS THE STRONG LINE

*"The market is below the 1-minute, 15-minute and 5-minute [50% lines] and above the 1 hour — that means the market is shrinking."* (056 · 00:29:09)
*"One hour is strong, super strong — one hour is long term. If it cannot break the one hour, the seller cannot take it further … so until then the market must circulate above."* (056 · 00:35:19–00:35:49)
*"5 minute is like a car gone down a 5-foot pit — five people together can push it up. 15 minute, a 15-step pit. One hour, a 60-foot pit — only 60 people together can take it up … it must come into its body-wick multiple times to go up strongly."* (056 · 00:38:52–00:39:23)

A state definition (contraction) plus a strength ordering of the 50% lines by timeframe (1m < 5m < 15m < 1H ≈ 1:5:15:60). A test of the 1H line needs several retests before a strong move. Compare T104 (O pattern, shrinking market).

### T443 — THE NEAREST (1m) 50% IS THE ROAD MAP; TREAT IT AS A BOUNDARY

*"Only if the market keeps maintaining beyond this 50% line can it go there; so the 1 minute is like a road map for us — 'now the market is in this direction'."* (056 · 00:30:11–00:30:42)
*"First it must break this 1 minute and put an outside candle — rather than just the 50%, this whole boundary, the 1 minute being small — only if it puts [a close] outside this and this is there a chance to go there."* (056 · 00:31:13)
*"One minute should never be taken [as a line]; one minute must always come as a boundary."* (056 · 01:00:49)

Operational filter: before targeting the 5m/15m lines, require an outside close beyond the 1m range (not only its 50%), and "maintain" beyond it.

### T444 — TIME WINDOWS: 1m HOLD FOR 4 h → TARGET IN 8 h; LOW BREAK + 3 h HOLD → 24 h OF DOWNSIDE

*"One minute is micro-short; in 24 hours micro-short is 4 hours. If within these 4 hours the market maintains beyond the 1-minute 50% line — maintained 4 hours, not just touching and going — then definitely within the next 8 hours the market will go into one place between the 5-minute and 15-minute boundaries above and touch it, or even break both."* (056 · 00:43:31–00:44:01)
*"If it breaks this low and puts an outside candle, I say long term: the next 24 hours the market is down — not an ordinary down, a terrible down."* (056 · 00:44:01)
*"If today it puts a red body beyond the body-wick, and for about three hours [the market] cannot break the 50% of that last candle, then the next 24 hours the market falls terribly."* (056 · 00:43:00)
*"If it breaks, then candle analysis for the next three hours; only if it maintains that for three hours will it go further up — to the 4-hour base or 4-hour 50% line."* (056 · 00:53:14–00:53:44)
*"If it travels below here for more than 8 hours, then definitely within the next 24 hours it will come here."* (056 · 00:51:11–00:51:41)

His first explicit time-boxes (for 24-hour markets, BTC/gold): hold ≥4 h beyond the 1m 50% → 5m/15m zone within 8 h; outside close through the day's low and 3 h without reclaiming the break candle's 50% → strong decline for 24 h. For NIFTY he says the far 5m/15m targets need "2–3 days" because the session is short (00:59:18–00:59:48). Refines T92.

### T445 — GROWING LARGEST CANDLE ACROSS PUSHES = BIGGER CANDLE ON THE BREAK

*"In the first seller push the biggest candle is this; in the second about the same; in the third a bit bigger; now in the fourth the biggest is here. So if it breaks this low today, puts an outside candle and keeps the market below for the next three hours, it will go down putting a candle bigger than this one."* (056 · 00:41:59)

*(heard once)*. Measure the largest candle of each same-direction push; if it grows push to push, expect a still larger candle after a confirmed break.

### T446 — TREND BOUNDARY vs PATTERN BOUNDARY

*"Trend is drawing one side; pattern is drawing two sides. If you draw from the extreme high to the low, that is called the trend; then the next extreme high-low — if you draw to that, it is the pattern."* (056 · 00:50:41)
*"Earlier I drew only for the overall trend; now I draw for the pattern … we get one additional boundary."* (056 · 00:32:14–00:32:46)

Two nested ranges per timeframe: trend range = visible extreme high to extreme low; pattern range = the latest swing (e.g. the V from the second extreme). Each has its own 50%.

### T447 — OVER VALUE ON EVERY TIMEFRAME = EXPECT SETTLEMENT / DOWN

*"It is above the 1 hour and above the 15 minute — above the 50% line means over value; 5 minute also has gone into over value … then there is only a chance for the market to settle and come down."* (056 · 00:58:48–00:59:18)
*"Above 1 minute, 15 minute, 5 minute, 1 hour — it stands above everything; so the market is in overall [over] value. If it goes up, how far? Draw the day or 4 hour and you'll know."* (056 · 01:00:49–01:01:20)

Mirror of T38 for selling/exits: price above the 50% of every trading timeframe → the upside is capped at the next higher-timeframe level (day/4H 50% or prior max body-wick) and settlement is expected.

Also corroborates: T100 (wick ownership — 00:08:14); T431 (fuel = volume; "price is the car, volume is the petrol" — 00:26:06); uptrend = higher highs and higher lows (00:21:29).

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T440** | On a retest: body past body only = 50/50; wick past wick only = 70/30 defender; neither = 100% defender and the level holds ≥ ~3 h; only a body past the wick is a takeover | **Yes** — 70 vs 80 figure unclear |
| **T441** | Two counter-moves retrace into the prior push; the third that stays outside starts the aggressive run (largest candle + 3+ same colour); run ends when bodies stop passing the wicks | **Yes** |
| **T442** | Below the 1m/5m/15m 50% but above the 1H 50% = contraction; the 1H 50% needs several retests before a strong move off it | **Partly** — "several" not counted |
| **T443** | Target the 5m/15m 50% only after an outside close beyond the whole 1m range and holding there | **Yes** |
| **T444** | (24 h markets) hold ≥4 h beyond 1m 50% → 5m/15m zone within 8 h; outside close through the low + 3 h not reclaiming the break candle's 50% → strong fall over 24 h | **Yes** |
| **T445** | If the largest candle grows push to push, a confirmed break brings a still larger candle | **Yes** — heard once |
| **T446** | Each timeframe has a trend range (extreme high–low) and a pattern range (latest swing); both 50%s are levels | **Partly** — "next extreme" chosen by eye |
| **T447** | Price above the 50% of every trading TF (1m–1H) = over value; expect settlement, upside capped at the day/4H 50% | **Yes** |

## Video 057 — Mother candle, "version 2" breakdown, same-timeframe candle reading, and the 3-line O pattern

He checks the previous day's BTC call (the low broke with a candle bigger than any since the mother candle and three straight reds). He defines the mother candle, says the trading system must change with the pattern type rather than use a fixed reward:risk, insists that candles be read on the timeframe whose boundary was drawn, and calls a market trapped between three timeframes' 50% lines an O pattern. About half the video (00:18–00:50, 01:02–01:04, 01:12–01:16) is about marketing, family and student stories (skipped).

### T448 — MOTHER CANDLE; A BREAK AFTER A FAILED 50% BOUNCE COMES AS "VERSION 2"

*"The highest high — we call it the mother candle … this is where the market starts; they are the ones who first sent back the buyer who had taken it this far, facing and fighting it with force."* (057 · 00:01:32–00:02:02)
*"If it cannot go even to about 50% of this, the market will break this low … if this low breaks it won't break ordinarily, it will go very aggressively: whatever biggest red candle it has put from this area to this area, there is a chance it goes putting one more of that size — its version 2 will definitely be put below."* (057 · 00:01:02, 00:02:02–00:02:32)
*"Another thing I said: three red candles will form continuously … equal to that biggest candle or bigger … the 1-hour control of fear has not yet come; it will definitely come."* (057 · 00:03:02–00:03:32)

Definition consistent with T36 (mother = the extreme candle where the move begins; re-marked when exceeded). Trigger: a bounce that fails to reach 50% of the last leg → the low breaks with a candle ≥ the largest same-colour candle since the mother, plus 3+ consecutive candles (T421/T430/T445).

### T449 — READ CANDLES ON THE TIMEFRAME THAT DREW THE BOUNDARY

*"Whose [boundary] is this — 5 minute? Then you must do the candle analysis of 5 minutes; whatever you draw for, you must do that one's candle analysis. Only then will there be clarity … like a child's shirt: whoever's size it is, they must wear it."* (057 · 00:57:06–00:57:36)

Procedural rule: a boundary drawn from the 5m high/low is tested with 5m closes (outside candle, body/wick), not with 1m or 1H candles.

### T450 — PRICE TRAPPED AMONG THREE 50% LINES = O PATTERN; CONFIRM THE BLAST WITH 3 CANDLES HELD OUTSIDE

*"Here is a 50%, here a 50%, here a 50% — the market keeps circling inside these three; so overall the market is in an O pattern. O pattern means the market is shrinking; its source is here and here — it will blast and go beyond this, or blast and go beyond that."* (057 · 01:06:48)
*"This is the boundary for the next four hours. Within the next four hours, only if the market puts an outside candle above all three of these lines and maintains for the next three candles — sustains — will it go to this source."* (057 · 01:11:22)

Corroborates T104 (O pattern → trade only the break) and adds the confirmation: outside close beyond all stacked 50% lines, then three candles holding outside; target = the range source (0 or 100).

### T451 — THE TRADING SYSTEM MUST CHANGE WITH THE PATTERN, NOT A FIXED REWARD:RISK

*"Many people say 'I'll just do it with 1:2, I'll do 1:3' — you cannot do it as one single mechanism; if you do business mechanically it won't work … you must change your flavour of trades according to the place, the situation, and the market's speed — is the market performing as a V pattern, an A pattern, an O pattern; is the market shrinking."* (057 · 00:07:10–00:08:41)
*"Technical-fundamental 20, trading system 20, fund management and psychology 30 and 30 … if those two are strong, even if your analysis goes wrong anywhere, you can bring any trade to profit."* (057 · 00:04:35–00:05:06, 00:07:10)

Negative rule for backtesting: a single fixed R:R exit is not his method; the exit logic depends on the pattern class (V/A/O) and speed. Consistent with T29 (entry 20%, management 80%).

### T452 — AFTER A 1H CONTROL-OF-FEAR LEG, A SECOND ONE STOPS AT THE TRENDLINE TOUCH POINT / SOURCE

*"If it breaks this, it will not go very far … where the trendline officially touches is this place; so there is a chance to come up to here … at this place the market, without giving a settlement, must come here, go up and fuel before it can go further down; it cannot go very far beyond, because the 1-hour control of fear was already put yesterday. Even if it puts another 1-hour control of fear, it will come only to this place."* (057 · 01:09:50–01:10:52)

*(heard once)*. After one completed control-of-fear leg on a timeframe, the next break is expected to stall at the nearest source/trendline point and to need a refuel (T431) before continuing.

Also corroborates: T434 (inside a zone of 1m/5m/1H lines it may refuel at any of them — "don't think it will do it only at this boundary; we'll be fooled" — 00:58:39); T428 (touch / not touch / break — 00:56:36–00:57:06); T46/T80 (₹1 lakh with 10× margin panics on one extra candle — 01:12:24–01:12:55); T437 (after control of fear ends, retail exits and the market rises smoothly back to the fuel point — 00:55:06).

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T448** | Mother = the extreme candle where the move starts; a bounce failing to reach 50% of the last leg → the low breaks with a candle ≥ the largest same-colour candle since the mother + 3 consecutive | **Yes** |
| **T449** | Test a boundary only with candles of the timeframe that drew it | **Yes** (procedural) |
| **T450** | Price circling within three TFs' 50% lines = O pattern; trade only an outside close beyond all of them held for 3 candles; target the range source | **Yes** |
| **T451** | No fixed R:R; exit logic depends on pattern class (V/A/O), contraction and speed | **No** — not specified enough to code |
| **T452** | After one completed control-of-fear leg, the next break stalls at the nearest source/trendline touch and must refuel first | **Partly** — the stall level is shown, not defined |

## Video 058 — Candle / pattern / trend continuation test, the two-50% rule, and the 25→50→source ladder

A 1H BTC review: after price touched the 50% line he lists three separate checks (candle, pattern, trend) that must all pass for continuation, and shows that only the candle check passed. He then reduces direction to two lines — the 50% of the buyer's leg and the 50% of the seller's current leg — and maps each level of analysis to a target (candle → 25, pattern → 50, trend → source). He also rates how close two 50% lines are. 00:39–01:18 is a long digression on schooling, jobs and politics (skipped), with a short gold/NIFTY recap at the end.

### T453 — CONTINUATION AFTER A 50% TOUCH NEEDS ALL THREE: CANDLE, PATTERN, TREND

*"If it is going up, three things must happen. By candle, it must be above the 50% of this current candle; … by pattern, it must have put a body close above wherever the previous maximum body and maximum wick are; then by trend, it must go above the 50% of this high and low. Only if it maintains all three can it go further up; otherwise it comes down — at least to the lower body-wick; if that breaks, to the source."* (058 · 00:09:14–00:10:45)
*"By candle it went beyond the 50%, by pattern it did not, by trend it could not … so candle is short term: the market moved up at short-term level; the chance of going further is very low — the buyer is weak, the seller is getting strong."* (058 · 00:11:16–00:11:47)

A three-condition gate (checked on the timeframe of the boundary, T449): (1) close beyond the 50% of the candle that touched the level, (2) body close beyond the prior swing's max body/wick, (3) close beyond the overall range 50%. Passing only (1) = a short-term bounce to fade.

### T454 — THE TWO-50% RULE: ABOVE BOTH = UP, BELOW BOTH = DOWN

*"If it is to be buy, when you draw what the buyer took, it must not go below half the distance they took. If the seller — it is going down — it must not go above half the distance the seller brought."* (058 · 00:13:47–00:14:17)
*"If it is going up, two things must happen: it must not come below this 50% of what the buyer took, and it must stay above this 50% line of the distance the seller brought … if it comes below both, it is going down … two Fibonaccis: if below both, the market is trying to come down; only if above both is the market going up."* (058 · 00:25:36–00:27:08)

Direction filter from two midpoints: M1 = 50% of the last completed buyer leg (past tense), M2 = 50% of the current seller leg (present tense). Close above both → long bias; below both → short bias; between → no call. A wick past a 50% is allowed; an outside close past it sends price to that leg's source (00:14:48–00:15:19). Formalises T97/T106.

### T455 — TARGET LADDER: CANDLE BREAK → 25, PATTERN BREAK → 50, 50 BREAK → SOURCE

*"If you do a candle analysis you can find out whether the market will go to 25; in this whole boundary there are 25, 50, 75, 100 — so whether it goes to 25 you can find out. With a pattern analysis — if the market breaks it — you can find out whether it goes to its 50. If it breaks the 50, you can find out whether the market goes to that trend's source."* (058 · 00:28:11–00:28:41)
*"If a candle's extension candle succeeds, at least it will touch the pattern; if they break the pattern, they will definitely at least touch its trend."* (058 · 00:20:57)

Numeric version of T429: a close beyond the last candle's body-wick targets the 25% of the range; a close beyond the pattern's body-wick targets the 50%; an outside close beyond the 50% targets the 0/100 source. Directly testable with T97 quarters.

### T456 — TREND = SIDE OF THE BUYER LEG'S 50%

*"Only while it is above this 50% line is it in the buyer's control … when it comes below it, the seller becomes strong — the trend itself becomes sell. Only while it is in over value is it a buy trend; if it comes to under value, it is a sell trend."* (058 · 00:29:12–00:29:42)

Corroborates T38/T438 with a binary trend label per leg.

### T457 — TWO 50% LINES CLOSE TOGETHER = A "ROPE" THAT HOLDS; FAR APART = PRICE RE-ENTERS AND ACCUMULATES

*"When two 50% lines come, you must see whether they are far apart or near. If very near, it has become strong — it cannot be broken. If far, the market will come inside it again and again — price will come, come, accumulate strongly and then go … if near, like a new rope, there is a chance the market proceeds further up without breaking that place."* (058 · 00:22:00–00:22:31)

*(heard once)*. Distance between two same-side 50% lines as a strength measure; refines T434 (zone). Threshold for "near" is not given.

### T458 — REPEATED WICK-ONLY TOUCHES (3–4) OF A BODY-WICK ZONE = THE ATTACKER IS SPENT

*"Each time like this it tried three, four times … if they try hitting it many times and cannot, it means the seller has become weak, the market has settled; then the buyer becomes strong. For the buyer to be strong: first it must go beyond the 50% of the maximum candle that touched; second, a body beyond the previous green body …"* (058 · 00:23:32)
*"A little peek beyond the 50% — we call that a failed candle; next, completely no body — that is called an indecision candle: no body, the wicks equal above and below; then this candle put an inside candle … for three hours the buyer could not take it beyond the 50% of what the seller brought — so the seller is still strong."* (058 · 00:15:50–00:16:21)

Corroborates T440/T424 with a count (3–4 failed touches) and names three candle types: failed (small poke beyond, no hold), indecision (no body, equal wicks), inside (T95).

Also corroborates: T425 (give candle analysis three candles — previous, current, next — 00:37:20); T433 (100 demo trades with him before 100 live — 01:13:28); T428 (miss, miss, then break on the third approach — 00:16:21–00:16:51).

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T453** | After a 50% touch, continuation needs (1) close beyond the touching candle's 50%, (2) body close beyond the prior swing's max body/wick, (3) close beyond the range 50%; only (1) = short-term bounce | **Yes** |
| **T454** | Above both the buyer-leg 50% and the current seller-leg 50% = up bias; below both = down; outside close past either sends price to that leg's source | **Yes** |
| **T455** | Candle break → 25% of the range; pattern break → 50%; outside close past 50% → 0/100 source | **Yes** |
| **T456** | Buy trend while above the buyer leg's 50%, sell trend once below | **Yes** |
| **T457** | Two same-side 50% lines close together hold (continuation); far apart → repeated re-entry | **Partly** — no distance threshold |
| **T458** | 3–4 wick-only touches of a body-wick zone without a body close = attacker spent; indecision = no body with equal wicks | **Yes** |

## Video 059 — Shrinking pullbacks, "power candles", the trendline midpoint, and the 6-month / 10%-drawdown money rules

BTC 1H/15m/5m/4H review of the previous call (the break came with a candle bigger than any green in the range). He shows pullbacks shrinking from source to 50% to 25% before a take-off, says a burst of large "power candles" is followed by a rest back to the resting point, and uses the intersection of the up and down trendlines as a buy/sell dividing line. 00:25–01:12 is a long talk on Marwari/Gujarati business training; two concrete money rules come out of it.

### T459 — PULLBACKS THAT SHRINK FROM SOURCE → 50 → 25 PRECEDE THE TAKE-OFF

*"First the buyer took it up; they came back right to the source … next they came only to its 50% … then it could not even go to 50 — now they turn at 25. At 25, again landing happens … then a new high … it is taking off."* (059 · 00:02:05–00:03:06)
*"It could come only to this 50; within the next three hours, if they keep maintaining below this 50 it is hard to go up again; likewise if they maintain above this 50, it will definitely go to this source."* (059 · 00:03:37–00:04:07)

Sequence claim: depth of successive retracements (measured in quarters of the prior push, T97) goes 100 → 50 → 25 → 25 before an impulsive leg; and a 3-hour hold on either side of a touched 50% decides the next target. Corroborates T106 and T421.

### T460 — A LEVEL TESTED THREE TIMES BREAKS ONLY ON FORCE; 3 HOURS HELD BEYOND = LOW CHANCE OF RETURN

*"The market came here the first time, here the second, here the third … to break this area now it is no longer a slow matter; only if there is tremendous buyer involvement in one force will it break fast."* (059 · 00:05:38–00:06:08)
*"If it breaks this place again, whatever biggest green candle was put between this low and this high, it will put another of that size or bigger … and the next three candles continuously."* (059 · 00:04:37–00:05:08)
*"Now they have clearly broken it; if in the next three hours the market maintains above, the probability of coming down again is very low — the buyer becomes strong."* (059 · 00:06:39)

Corroborates T448/T430 (break candle ≥ the largest same-colour candle in the range + 3 consecutive) and T444 (3-hour hold).

### T461 — A BURST OF "POWER CANDLES" = THE PUSHER RESTS; EXPECT A RETURN TO THE RESTING POINT FIRST

*"Because it put such big, big candles — one, two, three, four big candles — the market has no more force beyond this; the buyer pushed as much as possible with big force. They call it a power candle — this is called a power candle … otherwise the buyer will rest; the resting point is up to here. It comes to this area and only after that is there a chance to push up. Overall it must go up, but short-term, immediately going up is very, very unlikely."* (059 · 00:08:40–00:09:10)

*(heard once)*. After ~4 consecutive large candles, do not chase: expect a pullback to the resting point (the 50% of that leg, or the lower-TF 50%) before continuation. Complements T421 (the run itself) with the pause that follows.

### T462 — WHERE THE UP AND DOWN TRENDLINES MEET = THE MIDPOINT; ABOVE IT BUY TREND, BELOW IT SELL TREND

*"The place where both meet is this place … the midpoint of both; where this trendline and this trendline meet — that is the midpoint. That is the zone. It will come up to this place. So while above this it is buy trend; only below this does it become sell trend … there will always be a price line at this midpoint. If it breaks beyond this and also breaks the trendline that is resistance above, the market has no obstacle; from there it goes at demon speed."* (059 · 00:14:16–00:15:17)
*"The 4-hour trendline — the place where what the seller brought and what the buyer brought meet is this place. Whenever the market is above this, uptrend; whenever it comes below, downtrend."* (059 · 00:16:49)

New tool: draw the falling trendline off the highs and the rising trendline off the lows; their intersection (and the price line there) is the trend divider, and a break of the opposing trendline releases a fast move to the source "with three candles" (00:14:16). Relates to T14/T73.

### T463 — MONEY: START WITH 1–2× MONTHLY SALARY; 6-MONTH BREAK-EVEN; WITHDRAW 30% OF PROFIT; 3 YEARS

*"Six months is a very, very important period. That is why I said: don't borrow 5 lakh, 10 lakh and put it in. Start with one or two times your monthly salary. Take it smoothly for six months; after six months it will come little by little. Of the profit that comes after six months, take 30%. Leave it for three years. After that, whatever monthly salary you started with, this same business will give five times, ten times that every month."* (059 · 00:48:17)

Position/capital plan: seed capital = 1–2× monthly income, own money only (T46); no withdrawals for 6 months; then withdraw 30% of profits and compound the rest for 3 years.

### T464 — CAPITAL TEST: ANY 10% DRAWDOWN = DISQUALIFIED; GO BACK TO KNOWLEDGE AND PRACTICE

*"In execution two questions must be answered: one, your capital must never take a hit — in any situation, if your one lakh becomes 90,000, that's it, you are disqualified. In funded accounts they check all this."* (059 · 01:01:59–01:02:29)
*"If the answer to these two questions [is capital safe? is it growing?] is no, no — you are not qualified … stop this business here and fix what is wrong in your knowledge and practice."* (059 · 01:04:33–01:05:03)

A hard stop for the whole account: −10% from starting capital ends live trading until retraining. Compare T11 (no stop loss per ladder) — this is an account-level, not trade-level, limit.

Also corroborates: T454 (the buyer leg's 50% — may come to it, a body beyond sends price to the source body-wick — 00:23:57); T433 (100 demo + 100 live before execution — 00:59:27); "75 is aggressive over value" (00:16:19, consistent with T97).

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T459** | Successive retracements shrinking 100 → 50 → 25 → 25 precede an impulse; a 3-hour hold above/below a touched 50% sets the next target | **Yes** |
| **T460** | A level tested three times breaks only with a candle ≥ the range's largest same-colour candle; if held 3 h beyond, a return is unlikely | **Yes** |
| **T461** | After ~4 consecutive large ("power") candles, expect a pullback to the leg's resting 50% before continuation | **Partly** — "large" not quantified; heard once |
| **T462** | The intersection of the rising and falling trendlines is the trend divider (above = buy trend, below = sell trend); a break of the opposing trendline releases a fast move to the source | **Partly** — trendline anchoring by eye |
| **T463** | Seed = 1–2× monthly income; no withdrawals for 6 months; then withdraw 30% of profits; compound 3 years | **Yes** (as a capital-plan simulation) |
| **T464** | Stop live trading at a 10% drawdown of starting capital | **Yes** |

## Video 060 — Trendline-break odds (7 of 10 fail), escalating trendlines, "don't catch the knife", and the 1m→1H→4h→8h→24h forecast cascade

A review of the previous day's BTC read (the 4-hour scenario played out), followed by BTC 15m/5m/1m, gold and NIFTY. New points: price that lingers at a support loses it; a fast move is ending, so wait for the boundary and three candles; a broken trendline is redrawn on the next timeframe; and his count that 7 of 10 trendline breaks come back inside. Long digressions on notes-taking, family and faith are skipped.

### T465 — 7 OF 10 TRENDLINE BREAKS COME BACK INSIDE; ONLY BREAKS AT THE PRICE OF ATTRACTION FOLLOW THROUGH

*"It is not that if the trend[line] breaks the market will go up. After breaking the trend[line] the market can reverse from there and come back inside, breaking the trendline again. Out of ten, seven times it comes back inside; only three times does it go up. Which place? Only in the zone where the price of attraction is does it break and go up — not right away."* (060 · 01:01:46–01:02:18)
*"If you traded on all these trendlines — trusting the trendline — you would have lost; only this one trade goes to profit … without teaching this nature they say 'trendline broke, buy, buy, buy'."* (060 · 01:02:48)

A falsifiable base rate: ~30% follow-through on trendline breaks overall; the valid ones occur only at the "price of attraction" (under value, T38) after the V-pattern retracements have shrunk (50 → 25 → <25, T459). Qualifies T15 and T426.

### T466 — WHEN A TRENDLINE BREAKS, REDRAW IT ON THE NEXT TIMEFRAME; DON'T SLIDE IT

*"If you drew a trendline for 1 minute and it breaks, go up and draw one for 5 minutes; if that also breaks, draw one for 15 minutes … then 1 hour … trendline, price line, trendline, price line — it keeps going like this. Or you can shift the trendline down and down, but shifting it does not work that well."* (060 · 01:00:44–01:01:46)
*"Of these, the really effective one is not the price line — it is the trendline … however far it goes from the trendline, that fast it runs back toward it."* (060 · 00:54:36, 00:57:09)

Procedure: after a break, the next target is the 50% price line, then the next-higher timeframe's trendline (T14 anchoring on the extreme low/high). Corroborates T73.

### T467 — LINGERING AT A SUPPORT = THE DEFENDER IS LOSING

*"After touching the trendline, it must put a candle beyond this [boundary] in that same force — pada pada pada — put an outside candle beyond it, and only then look back. If it keeps dragging it here, chewing, the buyer's involvement reduces, the buyer's power and force reduce, the buyer's control in the market reduces. The seller will take control strongly. Then if it breaks here — sada sada sada — the market starts going down."* (060 · 00:29:38–00:30:40)
*"Touching the trendline and going up — only if it is like that will it go up. If it touches it twice and lingers, the buyer's confidence definitely weakens, the seller's increases, and the market drops with a thud."* (060 · 00:31:10)

Timing rule for a bounce: the reaction off a support must produce an outside candle beyond the local boundary immediately; repeated touches/sideways candles at the support predict its break. Complements T458 (which reads the same repeated touches as the *attacker* failing) — the difference is whether the bounce leaves quickly *(unclear how many candles count as "lingering")*.

### T468 — A FAST MOVE IS ENDING: DON'T CATCH IT; WAIT FOR THE BOUNDARY AND THREE CANDLES

*"However fast the market comes, it means the market is about to settle. Don't be afraid of a fast market. At the same time, a fast-falling market is like a knife falling from above — don't stick out your tongue to catch it … stand aside; when it falls to the ground, bend and pick up the knife patiently. Look at the boundary, see what it does at the boundary, do candle analysis and decide."* (060 · 00:36:17–00:37:20)
*"Look at what the fast market does at the boundary — inside candle or outside candle, what it does for the next three candles."* (060 · 00:37:20–00:37:50)
*"Because it came aggressively with big candles, like a ball falling fast from above hitting the floor, it goes some distance and comes back."* (060 · 00:23:01)

No entry during a vertical move; entry only after price reaches the next boundary and three candles there are read (inside/outside, T95). Consistent with T30 and T421 (climax → settlement).

### T469 — CASCADE OF FORECAST HORIZONS: 1m–15m → 1 h; 1 h → 4 h; 4 h → 8 h; 8 h → 24 h

*"Look: one, two, three green candles — this is the boundary. For the next quarter hour, half hour, at most 1 hour: while above this, up today; below this, down … analysing this gives the next four hours; with the four hours you get the next eight hours; analysing the eight hours overall, you can identify how the 24 hours will be."* (060 · 00:46:26–00:46:57)
*"If you analyse 1 minute, 5 minute, 15 minute you can see up to one hour; if you analyse up to one hour you can see the next four hours; the next four hours → up to eight hours; then the next 24 hours will be that way."* (060 · 00:47:27)

Refines T444 and T92: each timeframe's boundary is valid for a fixed look-ahead (1m three-candle boundary ≈ 15–60 min, 1H ≈ 4 h, 4 h ≈ 8 h, 8 h ≈ 24 h).

### T470 — A BODY THAT SLIPS OUTSIDE MUST BE RECLAIMED WITHIN 3 CANDLES; A SAME-CANDLE REJECTION IS A FAILED BREAK

*"If it puts the body outside, within the next three candles it must put the body back above this boundary … it must not break this low again … only if it puts the body back up without breaking does it go up fast; otherwise the seller becomes strong."* (060 · 00:32:42–00:33:12)
*"It came here, put an outside candle fast, but the next candle could not sustain; the seller could not maintain that place; in the same candle it closed back inside beyond the 50%, green again, and went back."* (060 · 00:25:04–00:25:35)

Corroborates T427 (3-candle re-entry window) from the other side, and defines a same-candle failed break (outside wick, close back inside past the candle's 50%).

Also corroborates: T457 (several 50% lines close together like a rope — hard to break — 00:15:23–00:16:25); T451 (no fixed points target: "sometimes it won't go 10 points, sometimes 100" — 01:04:18); T46/T80 (no margin or borrowing — 01:04:48–01:05:48); T463 (start with 2× monthly salary; about 3 years — 00:03:37).

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T465** | ~7 of 10 trendline breaks return inside; follow-through happens only at the price of attraction (under value, after shrinking retracements) | **Yes** |
| **T466** | After a trendline break, target the 50% line, then redraw the trendline on the next-higher timeframe; do not slide the old one | **Partly** — anchoring by eye |
| **T467** | A bounce off support must print an outside candle beyond the local boundary immediately; lingering/repeated touches predict the support breaking | **Partly** — "lingering" not counted |
| **T468** | No entry during a vertical move; wait for the next boundary and read three candles there | **Yes** |
| **T469** | Look-ahead per timeframe: 1m–15m boundary ≈ ≤1 h; 1H ≈ 4 h; 4 h ≈ 8 h; 8 h ≈ 24 h | **Yes** |
| **T470** | A body close outside a boundary must be reclaimed within 3 candles; an outside wick closed back past the candle's 50% is a failed break | **Yes** |

## Video 061 — V-pattern progression (failed → equal → extra), inside/outside patterns, trendline on two bodies, depth zones, and "extreme under value"

BTC review, then a refresher on V/A/O patterns: in a down-trend the V patterns are Failed, then Equal as the market settles, then Extra as it turns up; inside patterns show a balanced fight and the first outside pattern shows the aggressive side. He restates how to draw a trendline (touching more than one body), classifies depth by quarters of the higher-timeframe range, and introduces a new term, "extreme under value". Gold and NIFTY follow; ~00:45–00:57 is motivational (skipped). He also makes long-range gold/BTC forecasts (not recorded as rules).

### T471 — V PATTERNS PROGRESS FAILED → EQUAL → EXTRA; A FAILED V THAT TURNS PRINTS A NEW LOW, AN EQUAL V THAT TURNS BREAKS AGGRESSIVELY

*"Mostly in a sell trend the market puts a lot of failed V patterns. If the market is settling, it starts putting equal patterns; after that the market starts going up putting extra V patterns … first the V pattern will be failed, then little by little equal, then it converts to extra V."* (061 · 00:10:47–00:11:17)
*"Here, for the first time, the buyer's involvement put an equal pattern once … failed, failed, failed … and here for the first time it could not break the low."* (061 · 00:12:47–00:13:18)
*"If it turns back as a failed pattern, it will definitely break this low and put a new low. If it turns back after an equal pattern, it will break aggressively."* (061 · 00:13:48)

Regime sequence on T22's V types: count consecutive V's by type; the first Equal after a run of Failed V's, followed by a V that does not break the prior low, marks the turn; Extra V's (breaking prior A heads) confirm the up-trend. An Equal V that fails leads to an aggressive break (also at 00:58:36 for NIFTY).

### T472 — INSIDE PATTERNS = BALANCED FIGHT; THE FIRST OUTSIDE PATTERN = THE AGGRESSIVE SIDE; SETTLED WHEN INSIDE PATTERNS STOP BREAKING THE LOW AND THE HEAD BREAKS UP

*"This V pattern put [its low]; did the next one go below this low? No — it is inside; that is called an inside pattern … it started and went outside, but where did it close? Inside. All of these are inside patterns. Here it put a pattern below — this is the first time it put an outside pattern: buyer and seller fought strongly; beyond that fight the seller brought it aggressively."* (061 · 00:14:18–00:14:48)
*"Putting inside patterns only, unable to break the lower low, unable, unable — automatically it breaks this head and goes up. This means the market has settled and is going up pattern-wise."* (061 · 00:16:49–00:17:21)

Corroborates T105 and adds the settlement signal: a sequence of inside patterns failing to make a new low, then a close above the last A head (buyer involvement, T68).

### T473 — DRAW THE TRENDLINE FROM THE EXTREME AND STOP WHERE IT TOUCHES MORE THAN ONE BODY

*"The extreme low is this, the high is this; put the trendline straight at this high and pull it toward the trend; where it hits — here it hits — when it touches more than one body, stop it. I told you in the old videos how clearly the trendline must be drawn."* (061 · 00:17:51)

Refines T14 (anchored on heads) and T426: pivot at the extreme candle, rotate until the line rests on ≥2 candle bodies (not wicks). This is the one explicit construction rule in the batch.

### T474 — DEPTH ZONES BY QUARTER: 0–25 MICRO-SHORT, 25–50 SHORT, 50–75 MID, 75–100 LONG; STRUCTURE MATCHES DEPTH

*"Micro short term, short term; this 50 to 75 is mid term; 75 to 100 is long term. So even the 4-hour depth forms up to the long-term [zone]. If depth forms, a matching structure forms … if the depth increases, the height must also increase: dig 10 feet, 10 feet high; dig 20 feet, 20 feet high."* (061 · 00:25:58–00:26:28)
*"50, 0, 100 are very important boundaries; the 25-to-0 and 75-to-100 areas are places for the market to settle; the 50% is where it decides whether it stays buy or changes to sell."* (061 · 00:03:37–00:04:09)
*"However far it goes from the trendline, that much height; as much depth, that much structure … first single layer, then multiple layers, then two times, three times what it put before."* (061 · 00:18:51–00:19:21)

Labels a retracement's term by how deep it reaches into the higher timeframe's range (quarters, T97) and claims the rebound ("structure") is proportional to that depth. Consistent with T26 (moves in multiples of the pattern's height) and T96 (layers).

### T475 — "EXTREME UNDER VALUE": BELOW THE 50% OF 5m, 15m AND 1H → A BOUNCE TO AT LEAST ONE OF THEM COMES FIRST

*"If it is below three 50% lines, we will call the market 'extreme under value'. Note this — I am teaching this new today. Extreme under value means it has come very extreme: finished the 5-minute under value, beyond 15 minutes, 1 hour … it came to extreme under value within the trading zone itself — like an offer."* (061 · 00:41:49–00:42:19)
*"Below all three — 1 hour, 15 minutes, 5 minutes — the market is very much under value; the market must correct a bit and touch at least one of these three green [50%] lines; only then can it go further down."* (061 · 00:42:49)
*"There are three 50% lines — 5 minute, 15 minute, 1 [hour]. It will definitely come to one of these three; at minimum, first the price line [and] trendline."* (061 · 00:57:06)

Mirror of T447. Falsifiable: when a close is below the 5m, 15m and 1H range midpoints, the next move touches at least the nearest of those midpoints before a new low. Links T38 (buy under value) with T431 (refuel).

### T476 — A STRONG 4H BOUNDARY HOLDS ~90%; IF IT BREAKS, THREE CONSECUTIVE BIG 4H CANDLES FOLLOW

*"If it goes below this, gold will go down more aggressively … if the market breaks this low on the 4 hour itself, it will put four big candles — without stopping, three candles down on the 4 hour … three red 4-hour candles in a row. Note all this."* (061 · 00:34:09–00:34:40)
*"This is a strong boundary; the chance of going beyond it is low — 90% it will not go below this 4-hour boundary."* (061 · 00:41:18–00:41:49)
*"Look at the monthly — one, two … eight green in a row, eight months … then it falls vertically … I said it would definitely go 30%; in three months 30% came."* (061 · 00:36:14–00:36:44)

*(each heard once)*. Timeframe-scaled version of T430/T448: a confirmed break of a 4H boundary is followed by ≥3 consecutive same-colour 4H candles. The monthly remark (8 consecutive green monthly candles above the 75% → vertical ~30% fall within 3 months) is a single example. His BTC "−75%" and gold "−45% from the high" calls (00:35:10–00:38:47) are forecasts, not rules.

Also corroborates: T428 (at a price/trend line: no touch, touch, break — 00:22:52); T455 (price beyond 25 → next 50; trendline break → next price line — 00:58:36); T104 (O pattern = neither high nor low broken, shrinking — 00:08:44); "for buying we use the V pattern" (00:08:44).

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T471** | In a decline V's run Failed → Equal → Extra; the first Equal followed by a V that holds the prior low marks the turn; a failed Equal breaks down aggressively | **Yes** |
| **T472** | Consecutive inside patterns failing to make a new low, then a close above the last A head = settled, buyer involvement | **Yes** |
| **T473** | Trendline: pivot on the extreme, rotate until it rests on ≥2 candle bodies | **Yes** (codeable) |
| **T474** | Retracement term by depth in the higher-TF range: 0–25 micro-short, 25–50 short, 50–75 mid, 75–100 long; rebound height ∝ depth | **Partly** — "∝" not quantified |
| **T475** | Close below the 5m, 15m and 1H range midpoints ("extreme under value") → price touches at least the nearest midpoint before a new low | **Yes** |
| **T476** | A strong 4H boundary holds ~90%; a confirmed break brings ≥3 consecutive large same-colour 4H candles | **Yes** — heard once |

## Video 062 — Read only at 0/50/100, re-entry after a head break, overall vs individual depth, and the forecast-horizon matrix

He checks the previous day's call (price held the 4H body/wick exactly) and repeats that the technical part is 20% of the job. New or sharpened points: candle and pattern reading is done only at the 0/50/100 locations; after a V breaks the prior A head, not re-entering the old body-wick zone means an aggressive move; overall vs individual depth; and a table of how far ahead each kind of 5m/15m/1H analysis sees. About half the video is a casino/probability analogy and remarks on introverts (skipped except sizing and base-rate claims).

### T477 — READ CANDLES AND PATTERNS ONLY AT THE 0 / 50 / 100 LOCATIONS

*"I told you clearly: only at places like 50, 0, 100 must you do candle analysis — write this in your notes, summary, keywords — because the market settles only in such places. So what place is this? The body-wick of the 4 hour — that 0 or 100 place."* (062 · 00:18:28–00:19:00)
*"But where must all this be done — don't forget. You must not keep doing it for every 5-minute candle; don't pounce on every green; do it at those boundaries. That 0, 50, 100 is very, very important."* (062 · 00:26:08–00:26:40)
*"Beyond a certain point you should not downgrade [timeframes]; that does not work in the market."* (062 · 00:27:11)

A location filter for every candle rule in this batch (T424, T425, T440, T453): apply them only when price is at a range extreme (0/100, including a higher-TF body-wick zone) or at its 50%.

### T478 — AFTER A V BREAKS THE PRIOR A HEAD: NO RE-ENTRY INTO THE OLD BODY-WICK = AGGRESSIVE; REPEATED RE-ENTRY = SLOW

*"Officially this V pattern must first break this A pattern's head and put a head. Then, if without body or wick coming back into this place — inside the body-wick — it goes up without touching, it will break this place aggressively and go up terribly fast, in control of greed."* (062 · 00:25:08–00:25:38)
*"If it goes beyond this and this officially and goes without coming back inside, it goes aggressively; if it keeps coming back and going, it goes slowly, slowly."* (062 · 00:25:38)

Speed classifier after a structural break: count re-entries into the broken zone; zero re-entries → expect an T421 run; several → a grinding move. Complements T427/T470.

### T479 — FORECAST HORIZON BY ANALYSIS TYPE (5m candle ≈ ≤1 h; 5m pattern ≈ 1–4 h; 5m trend ≈ 1 day; 1H trend ≈ 3 days)

*"If you do 5-minute candle analysis you can see how the market will be from the next 15 minutes to a maximum of one hour; 15 minute [candle] — from 1 hour to 4 hours; the same 5-minute pattern analysis — from one hour to the next four hours; likewise 5-minute trend analysis — up to one day. If you do 5, 15 and 1 hour together — from minimum 4 hours to maximum 24 hours. If you take the 1-hour trend, you can see the next three days."* (062 · 00:28:42–00:29:12)

A concrete validity table for the candle/pattern/trend levels (T27 vocabulary), refining T469/T444 and T92: in a backtest, a signal of level L on timeframe T should be scored only within its stated horizon.

### T480 — OVERALL vs INDIVIDUAL DEPTH: A RETRACEMENT THAT REACHES 50% OF THE LAST V BUT ONLY 25% OF THE OVERALL LEG → STILL DOWN

*"Overall means from this high to this low; individual means up to where the last maximum high was … taking this whole depth is overall; taking this depth separately is individual. For the overall it went only to 25, not 50, 75, 100; for the individual it went up to 50. You must look at both."* (062 · 00:23:07–00:23:37)
*"The overall went only 25, could not go to 50; so the market must go further down."* (062 · 00:24:07)
*"A failed V pattern that could not go 25, 50, 75, 100 — it went only to 25; if it turns at 25 the market will go down even faster."* (062 · 00:22:37)

Formalises the "individual vs overall" vocabulary with a decision rule: the overall-leg quarter decides direction; an individual-leg 50% touch is only a refuel (T431). Consistent with T97/T106.

### T481 — SIZING IS FIXED BY THE LOCATION, NOT BY HOW RIGHT THE READ LOOKS; BASE RATES

*"Even if you give me 1,000 crore, for 1,000 crore I will put only what I must put at this place — not one paisa extra will I invest at this place. That is why money keeps coming to us permanently. You think: 'he reads the market so clearly, it happens as he says — let me put 10 lakh at once and earn 10–20 lakh in one trade' — that is the psychological weakness."* (062 · 00:52:07)
*"At the places where you think support or breakout is, 3 times out of 10 they will show it going up temporarily; 7 times out of 10 they come down — fast, with consecutive red candles, bigger than the candles before — and settle at the next boundary."* (062 · 00:08:16)
*"On average, every third trade you will be in profit overall; your capital keeps growing."* (062 · 00:58:18) *(unclear: "every third trade" — whether win rate or equity checkpoint)*

Corroborates T63/T83 (size from the formula, never more because of conviction) and T465 (7/10 of support/breakout signals fail, with bigger candles on the failure). Also: "before a trade, give the highest priority to finding why you should NOT trade here" (062 · 01:05:51) — a pre-trade veto checklist.

Also corroborates: T440 (at the 4H body-wick: wick past wick, body not past body, then a green candle — "about 80% buyer control" — then the next close above the prior candle's 50% ends the chance of breaking the zone — 00:19:00–00:21:05); T425 (1-2-3 candle analysis completes the read — 00:21:05); T46/T80 (the rich never use margin or borrow — 00:38:24).

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T477** | Apply candle/pattern rules only when price is at 0, 50 or 100 of a boundary (incl. a higher-TF body-wick zone) | **Yes** (filter) |
| **T478** | After a V closes above the prior A head: no re-entry into the broken body-wick → fast run; repeated re-entry → slow move | **Yes** |
| **T479** | Signal horizon: 5m candle ≈ 15 min–1 h; 15m candle ≈ 1–4 h; 5m pattern ≈ 1–4 h; 5m trend ≈ 1 day; 5m+15m+1H ≈ 4–24 h; 1H trend ≈ 3 days | **Yes** |
| **T480** | Direction is set by the overall leg's quarter reached; a retracement to 50% of the last V but only 25% of the overall leg → continuation down | **Yes** |
| **T481** | Never exceed the formula size at a location regardless of conviction; ~7/10 support/breakout signals fail | **Partly** — the win-rate phrase is unclear |

## Video 063 — Pattern analysis on BTC: 3-candle reclaim window, 50% tests, overall vs individual boundaries

Live BTC read (4H/Day/1H/15m/5m). He moves from candle analysis to "pattern analysis": whether each buyer bounce clears 50% of the prior leg, whether a new bounce clears the prior bounce's top ("depth vs structure"), and how long a side has to reclaim a boundary after a body closes outside it (3 candles of the timeframe). He then lays out 25/50/75 as short/mid/long-term gates and introduces drawing a second, "individual" boundary (75 → 0) on top of the overall one. Second half (from ~41:00) is psychology/sales, with one numeric claim about 100 winning trades.

### T482 — THREE-CANDLE RECLAIM WINDOW

*"If they put it outside beyond this, the next roughly three candles — three hours — is their time. Within these three hours go anywhere you like below; if you come back and put a close inside here, after that it is very hard to break down."* (063 · 00:14:48)
*"Once it goes inside within three hours, ... there is a chance to settle and go up."* (063 · 00:15:19)
*"If they go beyond this below, within the next three hours — one hour means three hours on the 1H, 4 hour means 12 hours — take it like that accordingly. If they come back, the buyer is active; otherwise it means it is very hard."* (063 · 00:17:53)
*"Only if the body goes beyond this is there the next three hours of time. ... Let it keep putting red bodies continuously; somehow within three hours if it returns back into this boundary, it means the market is coming into the buyer's control. Otherwise the market is in the seller's control."* (063 · 00:28:33, 00:29:04)

The boundary is the body/wick zone the buyer last launched from. A body close outside it is not yet a break: the defending side gets 3 candles of the same timeframe to close back inside. Reclaimed → the level becomes hard to break and a move the other way is favoured; not reclaimed → "100%" continuation (063 · 00:16:20). Refines T1/T20 (what counts as a confirmed break) with a time limit, and is the multi-timeframe form of T102's time-based exhaustion.

### T483 — WICK MAY ENTER THE BUYER ZONE, BODY MAY NOT; REPEATED VISITS = FAILURE

*"Those pulling it down must not come below this. Within this the maximum effort may come down even as far as the wick. They must make it a body within that same candle. That is why I said the wick below belongs to the buyer."* (063 · 00:27:33)
*"That too only two or three times. If they keep coming to this place again and again, it means the buyer could not do it. Then what happens? It breaks aggressively and goes further down."* (063 · 00:28:03)

Corroborates T100 (wick belongs to the other side). New: the defence has a count limit — beyond about 2–3 revisits of the same zone, expect the break. Compare T15 (trendline tested 2–4 times before breaking).

### T484 — A BROKEN 4H LEVEL PRINTS THREE MORE 4H CANDLES; DEPTH NEEDS THREE CANDLES OF THAT TIMEFRAME

*"I already said: if it breaks the four hour, the market will consistently be red on the four-hour candle for the next three candles."* (063 · 00:05:36)
*"If it breaks, it will consistently put the next three big candles on the four hour itself. But breaking this place is not ordinary, because ... the day's base is also in that place."* (063 · 00:06:06)
*"For the 4 hour and day's depth to increase, putting it ordinarily is not enough. If it puts three candles like a four hour or three candles like a day, the market's depth will definitely be much more."* (063 · 00:09:14)

A break of a higher-timeframe boundary is expected to continue for ~3 consecutive candles of that timeframe in the break direction. Also: if the break closes outside and holds for the next three hours on 1H, "99.99%" it goes down aggressively (063 · 00:08:42). Links to T482 (same 3-candle unit).

### T485 — BOUNCES THAT NEVER REACH 50% OF THE UPPER LEG = AGGRESSIVE SELL TREND

*"First time it came without being able to go to that 50. Again this V pattern ... not to 50 either. Next I put it on this V pattern — still not to 50. ... Up to now it has not gone. They call it an aggressive sell trend; it is in a frenzied sell trend."* (063 · 00:09:45, 00:10:15)
*"At least now it must go beyond this 50%; only then does the market say it is in the buyer's control."* (063 · 00:10:46)

For each successive V, measure the bounce against the 50% of the leg above it. A string of Vs all failing 50% = seller in full control; the first clear of 50% = buyer control. Corroborates and sharpens T97/T106 (applies the 50% test to every V in sequence).

### T486 — "DEPTH VS STRUCTURE": FIRST BOUNCE THAT CLEARS THE PREVIOUS BOUNCE'S TOP

*"Depth is this much; see, its structure did not go to this place ... The buyer could not take it to this point where the structure already was."* (063 · 00:11:16)
*"Now for the first time they have taken it beyond this ... before this they could not take the structure beyond that depth. ... automatically depth beyond depth came, and for the first time they have taken it beyond the previous structure."* (063 · 00:11:46)
*"Before this it was fully in depth's control; the structure existed only in name."* (063 · 00:13:16)
*"Now for the first time this V is small ... it has not broken the low; without breaking it, it has put a structure inside the already existing structure. That means the market is gradually coming into the buyer's control ... temporarily. That does not mean it must go up; only officially when they put it above this."* (063 · 00:12:46, 00:13:16)

"Depth" = each new low; "structure" = the bounce top from it. As long as each bounce stays below the previous bounce top, the seller owns the move. The first bounce above the previous bounce top, and the first V smaller than the previous one (no new low), are early buyer signs — but only "official" on a close above the prior high. Close to T103/T105 but adds the smaller-V (contraction) signal.

### T487 — A PULLBACK MUST HOLD 50% OF THE BUYER'S LAST UP-MOVE

*"Before this, how far did they take it up at most? From here to here. Then it must not come below the 50% of this. If it came below, the market has a chance to come to this source itself."* (063 · 00:28:03, 00:28:33)
*"The 50% of what the buyer took is here; if they maintain above this, safe."* (063 · 00:29:04)

The pullback after a buyer push is judged at the push's 50%: holding above → continuation; closing below → expect a full return to the push's origin ("source"). Same 50% logic as T97, applied to the pullback rather than the bounce.

### T488 — THE BIGGEST CANDLE IS THE LINE OF MAXIMUM STRENGTH

*"A big candle means maximum strength. ... If, beyond where they put that maximum strength, a red candle goes again, it is very dangerous — the market will go down terribly."* (063 · 00:31:08)
*"Only because they pushed giving their life is there this big candle. So beyond this the sell candle must not go down again; if it goes, the buyer is in trouble."* (063 · 00:31:39)

A close by the opposite colour beyond the body of the largest recent candle cancels that side's strongest push and signals a strong move the other way. Related to T101 (outside beyond the extreme candle brings an aggressive break).

### T489 — AFTER A BREAKDOWN, EACH NEW RED CANDLE OUTSIZES THE LAST V'S RED CANDLE

*"If it breaks without all this, the depth will go down terribly aggressively ... Look at the red candle in this V — a candle bigger than that will be here. Look at the red candle in this V — a bigger candle than that will go here."* (063 · 00:21:27, 00:21:57)

After a failed defence, the break's red candles are expected to exceed the largest red candle of each earlier V. Corroborates T2 (a red candle exceeding the largest prior red) as a continuation signature.

### T490 — AFTER THE LAST BOUNDARY BREAKS, CANDLES ~3× THE PRIOR BIG ONES *(heard once)*

*"Once it goes beyond this it will start going up very fast. ... Whatever big candles we have put so far, it will put candles three times that — one big candle plus consecutive green candles, control of greed — and the market will go up swiftly."* (063 · 00:18:24, 00:18:54)

Magnitude claim for the move after the final (trend) boundary breaks. Compare T108 (1.5–2× the last counter-move) — this is a candle-size multiple, not a leg multiple.

### T491 — 25/50/75 AS SHORT/MID/LONG-TERM GATES, PASSED IN ORDER

*"Keep its 25 and 75. It must not come below this 25. If it goes above this 25 the market is up in the short term. If it crosses 50 it is up in the mid term. If it crosses even the 75 it is up in the long term — in a buy trend in the long term itself."* (063 · 00:44:58)
*"Taking this as a boundary, 0 to 25 is micro short term, 25 to 50 is short term ... going beyond this is long mid term, 75 to 100 is long term."* (063 · 00:47:00)
*"It may come as far as here, but must not put a wick beyond it — must not put a body beyond it. If it does, this boundary expands a bit more."* (063 · 00:47:00, 00:47:31)
*"Only if it crosses 25 can it go to 50. Only if it crosses 50 can it reach 75. Only if it crosses 75 can it go beyond 100. That is the logic."* (063 · 01:04:27)

Assigns a term label to each quarter of the visible high–low (T97) and states they are cleared strictly in order. A body close back below 25 means the boundary "expands" (a new low is due). Refines T97.

### T492 — DRAW A SECOND, "INDIVIDUAL" BOUNDARY FROM 75 TO 0 — TWO 50% RESISTANCES

*"Next, automatically 75 to zero line. This is called individual — individual boundary. ... Draw from 75 to zero, that's all. Where is its 50? Here. It can reach the source only if it crosses both of these."* (063 · 00:48:33)
*"Officially where are the two strong resistances? One is here, second is here. Draw one by one. You must know how to see overall, and also individual. This I am teaching new today."* (063 · 00:49:05)
*"Many times, out of 10, about three times it turns without touching; the third ... touches with the body; the fourth breaks its 75 and turns."* (063 · 00:50:06) *(unclear — the counts are garbled)*

Besides the overall fib (0–100), draw a sub-fib on the 75→0 (or 50→0) section; its 50% is an extra resistance below the overall 50%. Price must clear both lines to reach the higher zone. He calls this new in this video.

### T493 — AFTER A FULL-FORCE 50% BREAK ON 5M, NO RETURN WITHIN 15 MINUTES

*"Only if it goes with full force like this can it break those 50% lines. Then within the next 15 minutes it must not come back beyond this. If it came back, crossing this again is hard."* (063 · 00:42:25)

The 5m equivalent of T482 with the window shortened: a real 50% break holds for three 5m candles. *(heard once)*

### T494 — BREAK FREQUENCY BY TERM *(unclear)*

*"This is the very short term, a 25% — okay. Out of 10, seven times it will break this place. But this place it breaks only three times in 10 — five times. This place, 3 in 10 only; the remaining seven times it goes up."* (063 · 00:42:55)

Claims the nearest (short-term) line is broken ~7/10 and the long-term line only ~3/10. The middle figure is garbled ("three ... five"). Testable only once the lines are defined.

### T495 — BELOW 50% FROM THE LIFETIME HIGH IS THE INVESTING ZONE; 50–70% IS WHERE BIG INVESTORS ACCUMULATE

*"If the market has broken 52%, its next target is only 70% — that is where the accumulation point is ... when it goes below that 50% the investing zone begins — if it goes 50% below from the overall lifetime."* (063 · 00:07:08)
*"The zone where such investors come is that 50 to 70 zone."* (063 · 00:07:40)

A higher-timeframe regime rule: a drawdown > 50% from the all-time high = investing zone; 50–70% is the expected accumulation band (his BTC call: 52–53% down, next 70%). Related to T38 (below 50% = under value).

### T496 — CANDLE ANALYSIS: THE NEXT CANDLE MUST CLEAR, THEN HOLD, THE PREVIOUS CANDLE'S 50%

*"Candle analysis means: this current candle must go above beyond 50%. ... The subsequent candles must not come below the 50% of this. ... This is candle analysis — current candle, previous candle — analysing three candles like this."* (063 · 00:16:51, 00:17:21)

Corroborates T20/T88 (50% of a candle as the reference) at the three-candle scale.

### T497 — 100 CONSECUTIVE WINNING TRADES BEFORE THE MIND "BELIEVES"

*"At least consistently, continuously, you must send 100 feedbacks of profit, profit to your mind; not even one of them must be sent as loss. You may send it delayed — no mistake."* (063 · 01:08:00)
*"If you send one loss feedback at the end after 30 profit feedbacks, all 30 get corrupted."* (063 · 01:06:58)

A training rule rather than a market rule: in the live-trading stage, sequence trades so there are ~100 consecutive closed wins with zero realised losses (a win may be delayed, never a loss taken). Fits T11/T45 (no stop loss, psychological reason).

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T482** | A body close outside a boundary is confirmed only if price fails to close back inside within 3 candles of that timeframe (1H→3h, 4H→12h); reclaim → reversal favoured | **Yes** |
| **T483** | Wicks may probe the zone, bodies may not; more than ~2–3 revisits of the same zone → expect the break | **Partly — "zone" is drawn by eye** |
| **T484** | A broken 4H/Day boundary is followed by ~3 consecutive candles of that timeframe in the break direction | **Yes** |
| **T485** | Successive V bounces all failing to reach 50% of the leg above = aggressive sell trend; first clear of 50% = buyer control | **Yes** |
| **T486** | First bounce above the previous bounce top, or first V smaller than the previous V (no new low), = early buyer sign; official only on a close above the prior high | **Yes** |
| **T487** | A pullback closing below 50% of the last up-move is expected to return to that move's origin | **Yes** |
| **T488** | An opposite-colour close beyond the body of the largest recent candle signals a strong move the other way | **Yes** |
| **T489** | After a failed defence, breakdown red candles exceed the largest red candle of each prior V | **Yes** |
| **T490** | After the last boundary breaks, candles run ~3× the size of earlier big candles | **Partly — heard once, "big candle" undefined** |
| **T491** | Above 25/50/75 of the visible range = short/mid/long-term up; levels are cleared strictly in order; a body close back below 25 means a new low is due | **Yes** |
| **T492** | Draw a sub-fib from the 75 (or 50) down to 0; its 50% is a second resistance that must also be cleared | **Yes** |
| **T493** | After a full-force 50% break on 5m, price must not return below it within 15 min, else re-break is hard | **Yes — heard once** |
| **T494** | Short-term line breaks ~7/10, long-term line ~3/10 | **Partly — counts garbled** |
| **T495** | Drawdown >50% from ATH = investing zone; 50–70% is the accumulation band | **Yes** |
| **T496** | The current candle must clear the previous candle's 50%, and later candles must not close back below it | **Yes** |
| **T497** | In live training, build ~100 consecutive closed wins with zero realised losses | **No — behavioural** |


## Video 064 — Supply/demand from buyer and seller counts; trading fast moves; BTC 1H re-read

About two-thirds of this video is about why he will not trade live on the channel, plus a long market-stall analogy: more sellers than buyers → sellers compete → price falls ("control of fear"); falling price draws in buyers ("price of attraction"). The method content: a counter-trade on a fast move must pay out inside that move's first 25% retrace; buy far enough below the big player that you exit before him; a level is broken on about the third visit. Closes with a 1H BTC read that repeats 063's 3-candle logic on 4H.

### T498 — AFTER A FAST MOVE, TAKE THE COUNTER-TRADE ONLY IF THE TARGET FITS INSIDE ITS FIRST 25%

*"A fast-moving market cannot go very far ... it must take a rest and only then can it go again, even in the same direction; it must put fuel."* (064 · 00:39:47)
*"The market has come only this far — only its 25 has come. Only if you can have a profit within that 25 should you attend that trade. Now think: to bring a profit within 25, how accurate must your entry be?"* (064 · 00:40:48)
*"That a fast market settles is true, that it gives some reverse is true ... but only if within that short distance you can bring your overall profit should you attend it."* (064 · 00:41:18)

After a one-sided run, expect a reversal of only about 25% of that run before it resumes. A counter-trade is allowed only if entry-to-target fits inside that 25%; buying after 3–4 confirming green candles uses up the room. Combines T97 (a 25% bounce leaves the seller in control) with T39/T41 (minimum target size).

### T499 — IF THE COUNTER-BOUNCE FAILS, THE HIGHER TIMEFRAME TAKES OVER *(unclear)*

*"Say on 5 minutes it has put continuous red candles like this. If it breaks that, it will put three or four candles on 15 minutes, or even directly on one hour. If on one hour it puts three, four or five big candles continuously like this, your story is finished."* (064 · 00:41:49)
*"You see how aggressively the market has come; it will not come back to this place again. Know this well — it will not come here. You must exit before that."* (064 · 00:44:51, 00:45:23)

When the bounce inside a fast 5m sell run fails and the run resumes, the continuation shows as 3–4 candles on 15m or 3–5 big 1H candles. Price does not come back to the failed bounce's entry, so the exit has to come first. Same "about three candles of the next timeframe" unit as T484. It is not clear exactly which level "breaks that" refers to.

### T500 — BUY FAR ENOUGH BELOW THE BIG PLAYER THAT YOU EXIT BEFORE HIM

*"Only if you have bought at a better place than him ... there must be a big difference between their buy price and our buy price. Only then will profit come to us before profit comes to him. If he decides to buy at 10 and sell at 12, only if we bought at 8 or 9 can we sell at 11 and come out ahead of him."* (064 · 00:43:20)

The exit has to come before the big player's distribution point, so the entry has to be deeper than his. This is the reason behind T38/T53 (enter where retail is afraid, below value).

### T501 — A LEVEL BREAKS ON ABOUT THE THIRD VISIT

*"It has come twice and gone. Now if it comes a third time — once it went without touching, once the body touched and went — if it comes again, it will break and go. When it breaks, from now on the four hour's forces will apply in the market."* (064 · 01:09:20)
*"Out of 10 ... about three times it turns without touching; ... it touches with the body; the fourth ... breaks its 75 and turns."* (063 · 00:50:06)

Visit sequence at a boundary: a miss, then a body touch, then the break. The break hands control to the next timeframe up. Sharpens T483 and T15 (2–4 tests before a break).

### Corroborations in 064

- **T482 on 4H:** *"If the market sustains up to the next three candles on the four hour, the market will come back up; if not, after that the story is finished — the four-hour candles will go terribly."* (064 · 01:09:51). A reclaim of about 12 hours after the outside candle (01:10:52).
- **T484:** he says his earlier "if it breaks here, 1H will put three consistent candles" call came true both times (064 · 01:09:20).
- **T11/T45:** *"Throw that 1:2 far away ... if you keep putting a stop loss everywhere, you go home with a towel on your head."* (064 · 00:33:41)
- **T67:** *"Out of 10, three trades do not go; only seven go, when done perfectly"*, and the three that fail move aggressively (064 · 00:43:51, 00:44:21).
- **T104:** long-term seller, mid-term buyer, short-term seller; the boundaries are converging, so *"the market is shrinking; it must blast one side"* (064 · 01:11:22).

| Rule | Statement | Testable as stated? |
|---|---|---|
| **T498** | After a fast one-sided run, expect only about a 25% counter-move; take the counter-trade only if entry-to-target fits inside that 25% | **Yes** |
| **T499** | If the bounce inside a fast 5m run fails, the run continues as 3–4 candles on 15m or 3–5 big 1H candles and does not revisit the failed entry | **Partly — the trigger level is unclear** |
| **T500** | Enter deep enough below the big player's buy zone to take profit before his distribution | **No — his price is not observable** |
| **T501** | Visits to a boundary go miss → body touch → break; the break hands control to the next timeframe up | **Yes** |


## Video 065 — Multi-timeframe boundary reading on BTC, Gold, NIFTY; trendline vs price line; empty space

Whole-market walkthrough (BTC, Gold, NIFTY on 1H/15m/5m, and 4H for NIFTY). New or sharper points: a "neck of the movement" level that sellers failed to break many times; a break climbs the timeframe ladder one step at a time (5m → 15m → 1H → 4H); empty, source-less stretches of chart get crossed fast; a simple "last opposite-colour candle" reading; and the difference between under-value by trendline and under-value by price (50%). The last 20 minutes cover fund management and SEBI rules on tips.

### T502 — "NECK OF THE MOVEMENT": A LEVEL SELLERS FAILED TO BREAK MANY TIMES

*"The market is at the neck of the movement — that means this place must not be crossed. Here you can see they have put multiple candles many times, about six or seven times the sellers tried to break it down. They could not."* (065 · 00:05:39)
*"If it crosses this it will come to this place very fast ... at least more than three candles, and the market will go up swiftly."* (065 · 00:06:43)

A level defended by one side about 6–7 times is a pivot. While price holds on the defended side, expect a fast move of 3+ candles toward the next boundary. Related to T15/T501, but here the level holds (the repeated failures are the attacker's, not the defender's).

### T503 — A BREAK CLIMBS THE TIMEFRAME LADDER ONE STEP AT A TIME

*"If it breaks the price line, the price line above; after that, upgrading to its higher frame, wherever the trend line is — it goes to that frame's trend line. That means if 5 minutes breaks, the 15-minute boundary; if 5 minutes' 50% is done, 1 hour, 4 hour — that is how the market travels, step by step."* (065 · 00:10:18)
*"If it stands beyond the 5-minute trend line, the 5-minute price line above is here; if it crosses that too, it will go to 15 minutes."* (065 · 00:12:49)

Targets are chained: 5m trendline → 5m price line (its 50%) → the 15m boundary → the 1H 50% → 4H. Each break's target is the next rung, not a fixed multiple. Refines T23 (5m → 15m → 1H escalation) for targets instead of adds.

### T504 — EMPTY SPACE (NO SOURCE, GAPS) IS CROSSED FAST
